### [1] [On quantum mechanics self-consistency: EPR incompleteness claims require no extraneous concepts beyond the theory's plain formalism for refutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10006)
*D. F. Orsini,L. R. N. Oliveira,M. G. E. da Luz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于量子力学的基本规则本身，仅利用EPR态（纠缠态）的两个核心特征——可观测量对应非对易算符以及测量所得关联信息的本质——就在不引入任何额外假设的前提下，反驳了EPR论证中关于量子力学不完备性的主张，证明了标准量子力学形式体系足以克服此类质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来学界重新审视量子力学自身的形式基础，关注其自洽性与完备性问题。EPR论证是历史上对该理论完备性的著名挑战，但现有反驳往往依赖技术结果和概念性异议，超出了纯量子框架。作者希望仅从量子力学核心规则出发，在框架内部解决EPR不完备性争议。

Method: 作者重新审视EPR论证，集中于其推理中两个关键要素：(i) EPR态（纠缠态）的可观测量总是与非对易算符相关；(ii) 通过测量获得的关联信息的本质。基于这两个要点，严格依赖量子力学的核心规则（而非任何外部命题），推导出对EPR不完备性主张的反驳。

Result: 作者展示了仅依靠标准量子力学形式体系，即足以推翻EPR论证中关于理论不完备的主张，无需借助任何额外假设或超出量子框架的命题。

Conclusion: 该结果在一个范例性场景中强有力地表明，量子力学在结构上具有自洽且形式基础健全的特征，标准形式体系本身就能解决对其物理适用范围的怀疑。

Abstract: From a phenomenological and experimental viewpoint, quantum mechanics is remarkably successful in explaining effects and processes in the microscopic world. Recently, however, a growing body of literature has revisited the theory's own formal foundations, specifically addressing issues of self-consistency and completeness. As a major historical example of these foundational challenges, the Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen (EPR) argument famously claimed that the theory is incomplete. While many valid criticisms and refutations of the EPR logic exist, they generally rely on a combination of technical results and conceptual objections to EPR's interpretive assumptions, thus transcending the plain quantum framework itself. Motivated by these trends of examining the theory's structural limits, here we revisit the EPR argument. Focusing on elements essential to the EPR reasoning, we first analyze general quantum correlations for EPR states: (i) the fact that their observables are always associated with non-commuting operators, and (ii) the nature of the correlated information obtained through measurement. From these two points alone, and relying strictly on the core rules of quantum mechanics, we demonstrate how to overturn the alleged EPR incompleteness without invoking any extraneous propositions. Consequently, we show that the standard formalism alone suffices to resolve such type of skepticism regarding the theory's physical reach. This offers, at least in a paradigmatic instance, a powerful indication of a structurally well-founded, self-consistent quantum theory.

</details>


### [2] [RF-Budgeted Frame Compilation for Frequency-Multiplexed Superconducting-Qubit Control Using Qubit-Control Identity Records and a Circuit-Informed RFSoC Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10013)
*Mingqi Ge,Anne-Marie Valente-Feliciano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套面向频分复用超导量子比特控制的、基于射频预算的帧编译与验证工作流，将QID记录、MATLAB/Simulink的RFSoC源链模型、QuTiP qutrit动力学与Qiskit算法工作负载相结合，在射频预算约束下对多音调RF帧进行调度、传播、解码和误差评估，并通过从单qutrit脉冲闭合到BV与QAOA微波层的系列仿真验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频分复用超导量子比特控制不仅是载波分配问题，共享源的射频预算会通过有限带宽、波峰因数、削波、量化、抖动、杂散、压缩、串扰和泄漏等多个机制扰动多比特旋转，而这些效应在现有编译流程中缺乏系统性的建模与验证手段。

Method: 该方法构建了四条基石的集成工作流：(1) QID记录编码比特特定的计算与泄漏跃迁频率、脉冲参数和驱动定标；(2) 基于MATLAB/Simulink的电路级RFSoC源链模型与有效串扰耦合矩阵作为编译输入；(3) 候选多音调RF帧在射频预算约束下调度，经RFSoC模型传播并解码为计算与泄漏跃迁帧；(4) 在QuTiP中进行qutrit动力学仿真，评估旋转误差、泄漏感知保真度、计算子空间存活率和瞬态泄漏。研究从单qutrit脉冲闭合推进到成对共存、多音调RF帧容量以及从Qiskit电路提取的BV和QAOA微波层。

Result: 仿真结果显示：较长脉冲改善每帧聚合，但未必最小化时间归一化的层代价；聚簇频率映射、较大旋转角和多音调泄漏叠加会加剧闭合误差。在标称射频预算下，从Qiskit派生的12比特BV-Y90层可在240 ns内以三个验证的四音调帧闭合，而QAOA混频器分区随旋转角和脉冲时长变化。

Conclusion: 所有结果均为基于模型的、无退相干假设的仿真诊断而非实测硬件保真度或布线缩减声明，说明该工作流提供了在射频预算约束下进行多比特微波层可编译性的预测性验证框架，但尚需硬件实测进行最终确认。

Abstract: Frequency-multiplexed superconducting-qubit control requires more than carrier assignment: the RF budget of a shared source can perturb multi-qubit rotations through finite bandwidth, crest factor, clipping, quantization, jitter, spurs, compression, crosstalk, and leakage. We present an RF-budgeted frame-compilation and validation workflow that combines qubit-control identity (QID) records, a MATLAB/Simulink-based circuit-informed RFSoC source-chain model, QuTiP qutrit dynamics, and Qiskit-derived algorithm workloads. QID records encode qubit-specific computational and leakage transition frequencies, pulse parameters, and drive-scale calibration, while the RF-chain profile and effective crosstalk-coupling matrix are provided as separate compiler inputs. Candidate multitone RF frames are scheduled under RF-budget constraints, propagated through the RFSoC model, decoded into computational and leakage transition frames, and evaluated in QuTiP for rotation error, leakage-aware fidelity, computational-subspace survival, and transient leakage. The studies progress from single-qutrit pulse closure to pairwise coexistence, multitone RF-frame capacity, and Bernstein-Vazirani (BV) and QAOA microwave layers extracted from Qiskit circuits. The simulations show that longer pulses improve per-frame aggregation but do not necessarily minimize time-normalized layer cost; clustered frequency maps, larger rotations, and multitone leakage stacking tighten closure. Under the nominal RF budget, a Qiskit-derived 12-qubit BV -Y90 layer closes in three validated four-tone frames at 240 ns, while QAOA mixer partitions vary with rotation angle and pulse duration. All reported results are model-based, decoherence-free simulation diagnostics rather than measured hardware fidelities or wiring-reduction claims.

</details>


### [3] [Local Complex Dependence and Separability in Madelung Hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10019)
*Lorenzo Pirovano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多粒子纯态波函数在固定位置表象下的交叉粒子混合导数，揭示了其与Holland-Wang局域依赖函数和Madelung速度场交叉Jacobian的联系，并证明了在无节点乘积区域上，交叉块消失等价于局域乘积可分离性。在含实标量势的薛定谔演化下，可分离态交叉块的首阶增长由势的混合Hessian驱动且为纯虚数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的纠缠度量通常是基无关的，缺乏局域可分离性和依赖性的诊断工具。作者希望构造一种局域的、位置表象下的可分离性判据，同时建立波函数对数的交叉导数与已知物理量（Holland-Wang局域依赖函数、Madelung速度场）之间的数学联系。

Method: 在固定位置表象下，对多粒子纯态的波函数对数取混合交叉粒子导数。将其实部分解为构型密度的Holland-Wang局域依赖函数的一半，虚部分解为Madelung速度场的交叉Jacobian。在无节点乘积区域上分析交叉块消失与局域乘法可分离性的等价性，并在薛定谔演化的框架下解析交叉块对时间的一阶发展。

Result: 证明了在无节点乘积区域上，所有交叉块在整个区域上的消失等价于局域乘法可分离性。在含实标量势的薛定谔演化下，从可分离态出发的交叉块初始增长由势的对应混合Hessian决定，且首阶时间项为纯虚数。

Conclusion: 该构造是一种局域可分离性与依赖性诊断工具，而非基无关的纠缠度量，补充了传统全局纠缠度量的局域视角。

Abstract: For a many-particle pure state in a fixed position representation, we consider the mixed cross-particle derivatives of the logarithm of the wave function. Their real part is one half of the Holland-Wang local dependence function of the configuration density, while their imaginary part is the cross-Jacobian of the Madelung velocity field. On a node-free product region, vanishing of all cross blocks throughout the region is equivalent to local multiplicative separability. Under Schrodinger evolution with a real scalar potential, the initial growth of a cross block from a separable state is sourced by the corresponding mixed Hessian of the potential and is purely imaginary to first order in time. The construction is a local separability and dependence diagnostic, rather than a basis-independent entanglement measure.

</details>

*M. W. AlMasri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了有限容量热库远离平衡态时，温度编码计算的平均错误概率、信道容量与熵产生之间的严格信息论界限，证明最小耗散在错误概率接近有限热涨落极限时发散，并揭示了级联网络中耗散随网络深度的基本开销规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主量子热机被提出作为基于物理的计算基底，其中逻辑输入输出以温度梯度编码（"热力学神经元"）。此类系统虽已展示计算保真度与热耗散之间的权衡，但其温度编码计算的基础信息论极限尚未被刻画，缺乏严格的界限来指导设计。

Method: 从随机热力学和有限时间信息论出发，对远离平衡态的有限容量热库建立严格的数学界限，推导平均错误概率、信道容量与熵产生之间的关系；通过证明最小耗散在趋近最小错误地板时发散，以及分析级联网络中的耗散累积，建立热力学信道容量饱和机制，并评估噪声放大条件下随网络深度增长的耗散开销。

Result: 证明了实现目标平均错误概率所需的最小耗散在错误概率趋向有限热涨落强加的最小错误地板时发散；建立了在高耗散下饱和的热力学信道容量；量化了级联网络中维持目标保真度所需的最小耗散，证明存在O(L ln L)的基础开销，且在强噪声放大条件下耗散需求可增长至O(L^3)。

Conclusion: 该框架弥合了随机热力学、有限时间信息论与自主计算之间的鸿沟，为能效优化的模拟热力学硬件提供了严格的设计原则，揭示了热涨落、信息容量与耗散之间的根本性折衷关系。

Abstract: Autonomous quantum thermal machines have recently been proposed as physics-based computing substrates where logical inputs and outputs are encoded in temperature gradients. While such ``thermodynamic neurons'' exhibit a clear trade-off between computational fidelity and heat dissipation, the fundamental information-theoretic limits of temperature-encoded computation remain uncharacterized. Here, we derive rigorous bounds linking average error probability, channel capacity, and entropy production for finite-capacity thermal reservoirs operating far from equilibrium. We prove that the minimal dissipation required to achieve a target average error probability $\langle ξ\rangle$ diverges as $\langle ξ\rangle$ approaches a fundamental minimum error floor $\epsMin$ imposed by finite-reservoir thermal fluctuations. We further establish a thermodynamic channel capacity that saturates at high dissipation, and quantify the minimal dissipation required for cascaded networks to maintain target fidelity, demonstrating a fundamental $\mathcal{O}(L \ln L)$ overhead with network depth, with the required dissipation growing up to $\mathcal{O}(L^3)$ under strong noise amplification conditions. Our framework bridges stochastic thermodynamics, finite-time information theory, and autonomous computation, providing rigorous design principles for energy-efficient analog thermodynamic hardware.

</details>

*Liang Luo,Shun-Cai Zhao*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放多体量子电池中，相干能量回流与非马尔可夫振荡会限制能量保持并降低可提取功，阻碍量子电池在实际硬件平台上的储能性能提升。现有无辅助的双体结构（充电器-电池）无法有效抑制这类耗散动力学导致的性能退化。

Method: 采用开放系统Lindblad主方程，构建集体自旋阵列量子电池与激光驱动充电器对称耦合到离共振辅助催化模式的动力学模型。通过数值模拟对比无辅助双体设置与催化介导设置的能量传递行为，并解析催化剂的微观机制（虚激发产生有效复数子系统间耦合J_eff，诱导欠阻尼到过阻尼的动力学交叉及选择性相干阻尼）。

Result: 数值结果表明：无辅助双体设置呈现明显的回流振荡和较差能量保持；催化介导可淬灭瞬态振荡并加速能量注入。催化剂保持恒定能量期望值<H_C(t)>≈<H_C(0)>且瞬态布居可忽略。有效复数耦合J_eff诱导欠阻尼到过阻尼的动力学交叉，防止电池布居耗尽，稳定粒子数反转，并显著提升渐近稳态ergotropy且随电池尺寸N_B增大而增加。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了催化剂介导能量传递的耗散动力学机制，为在现代量子硬件平台上提升储能稳定性提供了切实可行的方案，拓展了对开放量子电池动力学控制的理论认识。

Abstract: Coherent energy backflow and non-Markovian oscillations limit energy retention and degrade extractable work (ergotropy) in open many-body quantum batteries. Here, we present a catalyst-mediated charging protocol for a collective spin-array quantum battery coupled to a laser-driven charger. Using the open-system Lindblad master equation, we examine the energy transfer dynamics when both charger and battery are symmetrically coupled to an off-resonant auxiliary catalytic mode. Numerical simulations reveal that while unassisted bipartite setups exhibit pronounced backflow oscillations and poor energy retention, catalytic mediation quenches transient oscillations and accelerates energy injection. The auxiliary system operates as an energy-invariant conduit, maintaining a constant energy expectation value $\langle H_C(t)\rangle \approx \langle H_C(0)\rangle$ and negligible transient population throughout the evolution. Microscopically, virtual excitations of the catalyst generate an effective complex inter-subsystem coupling $J_{\text{eff}}$, which induces an underdamped-to-overdamped dynamical crossover and introduces selective coherence damping. This mechanism prevents population depletion in the battery, stabilizing the population inversion and significantly increasing the asymptotic steady-state ergotropy with increasing battery size $N_B$. These findings clarify the dissipative dynamics of catalyst-mediated energy transfer and provide a practical scheme for improving storage stability in modern quantum hardware platforms.

</details>


### [6] [Global Non-Identifiability of Fubini-Study Geometry from Complete One-Period Endpoint Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10051)
*Rashid Ahmad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了周期驱动有限维量子系统中，由所有起始时间和外部参数索引的单周期端点数据（单演矩阵路径）在一般情况下无法重建固定初始态的周期平均Fubini-Study度量分量。障碍被精确刻画：端点数据只决定单演矩阵的共轭路径，而非其酉提升；观测纤维恰为单演矩阵逐点中心化子上的右作用轨道。文章通过显式的实解析二能级例子证明了该非可辨识性，且该结论在哈密顿量导数有界时依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期驱动量子系统的完整单周期端点观测数据（包含所有起始时间和外部参数）是否足以确定周期内的几何量（如周期平均Fubini-Study度量）是一个基础的信息论/可辨识性问题。已有工作通常关注相位或Floquet对数等自由度，但本文揭示了更深层的信息缺口：酉提升而非标量相位，这一障碍的精确刻画对量子信息、量子计量和周期驱动量子模拟的观测设计具有重要意义。

Method: 作者在光滑周期哈密顿量类上建立严格的非可辨识性定理：(1) 证明起始时间索引的端点数据决定单演矩阵的共轭路径（即观察到的monodromy路径），但不决定其特定的酉提升；(2) 在每个固定monodromy切片上刻画观测纤维，即由单演矩阵逐点中心化子生成的平滑参数依赖的基环右作用轨道；(3) 证明Fubini-Study泛函在该纤维作用下不保持不变，因此不能通过端点观测映射因子化；(4) 构造一个实解析的二能级通勤单生成元哈密顿族作为显式反例，展示无需非阿贝尔时间排序或Floquet对数歧义即可出现障碍；(5) 证明在哈密顿量参数导数任意给定一致上界下非可辨识性仍成立。

Result: 得到了一个确定性的全局非可辨识定理：完整的起始时间解析单周期端点数据不足以确定周期平均Fubini-Study度量。障碍的精确来源是缺失的酉提升信息而非标量相位。给出了一个二能级实解析显式例子，其中连续一族哈密顿量产生完全相同的端点数据但不同的Fubini-Study几何（在不限制类上可任意分离）。该结论在参数导数有界的约束下依然成立。

Conclusion: 该结果是确定性的全局性声明，而非一般性非可辨识或实验不可行性声明。它精确定位了完整单周期端点数据与完整周期内动力学之间的信息缺口：缺失的信息是观测到的monodromy路径的酉提升，而非普通的标量相位自由度。这一结论对周期驱动量子系统的观测方案设计和可辨识性分析具有直接的指导意义。

Abstract: We establish a global, worst-case non-identifiability theorem for periodically driven finite-dimensional quantum systems. On the unrestricted smooth periodic Hamiltonian class, we show that the period-averaged Fubini-Study metric component of a fixed initial state cannot, in general, be reconstructed from exact one-period propagators indexed by every starting time and external parameter. Thus, even complete starting-time-resolved endpoint data are insufficient to determine this intra-period geometric quantity. The obstruction is characterized exactly. The starting-time-indexed endpoint data determine the conjugation path of the monodromy, but not its particular unitary lift. On each fixed-monodromy slice, the observational fibres are precisely the right orbits generated by smooth parameter-dependent based loops taking values in the pointwise centralizer of the monodromy. The Fubini-Study functional is not invariant under this fibre action and therefore does not factor through the endpoint observation map. An explicit real-analytic two-level witness demonstrates the obstruction within a commuting, one-generator Hamiltonian family, so neither non-Abelian time ordering nor Floquet-logarithm ambiguity is required. A continuous family of Hamiltonians produces identical starting-time-indexed one-period endpoint data while yielding different, and on the unrestricted class arbitrarily separated, period-averaged Fubini-Study geometry. Non-identifiability persists under any prescribed uniform bound on the parameter derivative of the Hamiltonian. The result is a deterministic global statement, not a claim of generic non-identifiability or experimental impossibility. It identifies a precise information gap between complete one-period endpoint data and full intra-period dynamics: the missing information is the unitary lift of the observed monodromy path rather than ordinary scalar phase freedom.

</details>


### [7] [ND-Photonic QRNGs in a Noisy Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10053)
*J. M. Agüero Trejo,Cristian S. Calude,O. C. Stoica*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种基于Loc-Kochen-Specker定理的3D量子随机数发生器（QRNG）的光子实现方案。与低温超导实现相比，该方案更易于部署。论文将光子3D QRNG建模为开放量子系统，由存在损耗的非理想分束器构成，并证明在特定条件下，即使存在测量误差，该过程仍处于Kochen-Specker定理的适用范围，从而保证输出的最大不可预测性。

Abstract: We study incomplete sets of orthogonal stabiliser states that cannot be extended by a further stabiliser state orthogonal to all of its members. Leveraging the symplectic geometry underlying the $n$-qudit Pauli group in prime local dimension, we explicitly construct USBs for systems of four qubits and three qudits of odd prime local dimension. Moreover, we show that these are the respective minimal qubit, respectively qudit numbers for which such bases exist, and that USBs exist for all $n\geq4$ qubit and for all $n\geq3$ odd-prime-dimensional qudit systems. Finally, we compare the resource-theoretic aspects of USBs with those of UPBs. We establish that, analogous to the case of UPBs, the orthogonal complement of every unextendible stabiliser set is a stabiliser-free subspace, and its normalised projector is necessarily magic; moreover, it is bound magic in odd prime dimension, yet need not be for qubits. Abstract: Multiparameter estimation remains a fundamental challenge in quantum estimation theory because the optimal measurements associated with different parameters are generally incompatible. In this work, we develop a Bayesian framework for the simultaneous estimation of multiple parameters. The spectral decomposition of the resulting operator defines a parametrized projection-valued measure. To determine their optimal values, we formulate a minimax optimization problem based on the normalized Bayesian mean-square errors, introducing a min-max normalization procedure that enables a meaningful comparison of estimation errors associated with different parameters. This optimization yields a variationally optimized projective measurement for simultaneous Bayesian estimation. Two qubit examples, involving phase estimation and the estimation of parameters defining convex combinations of unitary operations, demonstrate the construction of optimized projective measurements and the corresponding minimum mean-square error estimators within the proposed framework for finite data sets.

Abstract: Classical identification via a noisy channel is a communication task in which the receiver is not required to reconstruct the full transmitted message, but only to decide whether it coincides with a message of interest. This relaxation allows the number of identifiable messages to grow doubly exponentially with the blocklength. For quantum channels, the resulting (doubly exponential) identification capacity $C_{\mathrm{ID}}$ can strictly exceed the ordinary (exponential) transmission capacity $C$.

For sufficiently low-noise channels, this bound can also be achieved via the Hayden-Winter (quantum) identification + fingerprinting codes. This yields an exact characterization $C_{\mathrm{ID}}=C_E$ of identification capacity for such channels. However, for general channels, we prove that this upper bound can be strict. We exhibit an explicit family of transpose-depolarizing channels for which $C_{\mathrm{ID}}<C_E$. As a consequence, we also obtain the first example of strict superadditivity of the identification capacity $C_{\mathrm{ID}}$.

Abstract: We study the role of ancillary entanglement in quantum channel parameter estimation and investigate the minimal ancilla dimension required to achieve the maximum Fisher information. Abstract: Quantum sensors promise measurement sensitivities that can scale at the Heisenberg limit, but in practice their performance is often degraded by noise, finite sampling, and implementation imperfections. In this work we present a general framework for improving parameter estimation in such settings by exploiting intrinsic structural constraints of time-domain correlation functions. Our approach builds on the observation of Kemper et al. [PRL 132, 160403 (2024)] that two-time correlation functions of Hermitian observables generate Gram matrices that are positive semidefinite, a property that can be violated in experimentally acquired data. We formulate signal reconstruction as a convex optimization problem that enforces positive semidefiniteness, Toeplitz structure, and low-rank priors motivated by the underlying dynamics. We show analytically that, under suitable conditions, the ground-truth signal can be uniquely identified in the noiseless case and recovered stably in the presence of noise. We further demonstrate numerically, in a GHZ-based magnetometry protocol, that enforcing these physical constraints can significantly improve frequency estimation from sparse and noisy data. In particular, we observe a clear advantage in the data-starved regime, where only a small number of time samples are available and standard spectral estimation methods, including matrix pencil techniques, provide limited or unstable improvement over direct fitting. While the reconstructed signals do not in general reach the shot-noise-limited performance, the proposed approach consistently reduces estimation error and recovers much of the underlying structure of the signal. These results indicate that incorporating universal physical constraints into data analysis can enhance the practical performance of quantum sensing protocols without requiring additional hardware resources or calibration.

This calibration is fixed before Bell-test data are collected. The displacement activates the odd modulo-four sectors, so their fixed a priori assignments are a genuine finite-energy component. The large gain is specific to the deterministic phase-bit coarse-graining; independently calibrating the one-bit POVM with randomized odd-sector outcomes gives only a much smaller improvement. These are honest-model predictions, not loss, detection-efficiency, or finite-sample thresholds. In an experiment, a device-independent guarantee for an extracted Bell pair follows by inserting a confidence lower bound on the observed CHSH score into the self-testing theorem.

We prove that every four-player XOR game with commuting-operator value one and at most three active questions per player has a perfect GHZ-equatorial strategy. Conversely, we construct a uniform eight-clause game with four active questions per player whose commuting-operator value is one but whose phase equations are inconsistent. Thus four is the sharp local-question threshold. Abstract: We study the sample complexity of estimating the von Neumann entropy of an unknown $d$-dimensional quantum state. All previously known estimators require $Ω(d^2)$ samples, and plug-in estimators are known to face a quadratic barrier. We give the first subquadratic-sample estimator: for additive error $\varepsilon$, our estimator uses
  \[
  O\!\left(\frac{d^2 \log^2(\log(d)) \log(1/\varepsilon)}{\varepsilon^2 \log^2(d)} + \frac{\log^2(d/\varepsilon)}{\varepsilon^2}\right)
  \] samples. In particular, for constant $\varepsilon$, the complexity is $O_\varepsilon(d^2\log^2(\log(d))/\log^2(d))=o(d^2)$. Our analysis introduces a new pinching inequality that bounds the entropy loss under a space direct-sum decomposition, together with a bias-corrected estimator for large eigenvalues and a new bounded-coefficient polynomial estimator for small eigenvalues.

No analogous code switching protocols exist for many other prominent families, such as rotated surface codes, or for finer $Z$-rotation gates. Furthermore, we show that this framework extends beyond color codes; specifically, it enables the generation of $r$-orthogonal quantum codes, $r \ge 2$, that inherit the local geometry of rotated surface codes. Finally, we extend the fault-tolerant code switching protocol based on transversal CNOT gates to incorporate fault-tolerant realization of $Z$-rotation gates at any level of the Clifford hierarchy for geometries compatible with rotated surface codes. Abstract: We present a formulation of quantum field theory based entirely on real numbers, which we call real quantum field theory (RQFT). The construction is obtained from the standard complex-number formulation by replacing the imaginary unit i with a matrix J throughout all formulas. Here J is the real 2x2 matrix satisfying the condition J^2=-1. This extends the recently developed formulation of real quantum mechanics based on the real Kähler space to systems with infinitely many degrees of freedom. As the basic example, we construct the RQFT for a scalar field. We define a field operator acting on the real Kähler analogue of the bosonic Fock space. This operator satisfies the Klein-Gordon equation and the J-form of canonical commutation relation. To construct the RQFT we develop the corresponding J-calculus. In particular, we introduce the direct and inverse J-Fourier transforms and the associated J-valued distributions. In RQFT, the usual unitarity condition for the complex S-matrix is replaced by the statement that the real scattering operator is both orthogonal and symplectic. The physical observables in RQFT coincide with those of ordinary QFT. Thus RQFT does not change the physical predictions of scalar QFT, but provides an equivalent formulation. We explore the possibility of purely real formulations of the Standard Model of elementary particles. We show that it does admit this formulation and the resulting theory has ortho-symplectic symmetry. The choice of the Standard Model as the testbed for exploring the possibility of purely real formulations is related with the fact it provides a realistic description of all known fundamental particles. The real formulation of the Standard Model naturally suggests a possible exit beyond the Standard Model. In particular, we consider the implications of breaking the J-symmetry as a marker for new physics.

In both models, the recursion functions capture the overall qualitative behavior of the recursion coefficients, and the gradient catastrophes of the recursion functions are associated with ``chaotic'' transition regions in the recursion coefficients. For the quartic potential, such regions can occur in both $R_n$ and $S_n$, whereas the DSSYK model can exhibit multiple transition regions in $R_n$, with the recursion function remaining accurate in the smooth intervals between them. Abstract: We construct a toy model of an evaporating black hole using approximately local degrees of freedom acting on ``overlapping" qubits in which a version of black hole complementarity arises naturally. Method: 作者简化了 SoS（平方和）松弛的舍入方法，推广了经典的"全局相关性舍入"（global correlation rounding）技术。利用这些技术，还证明了"pinning lemma"的一个新变体——这是一个在 LP/SDP 舍入、高维概率和统计物理中被广泛使用的测度分解定理。

Result: 给定 BSS(M)=1 的矩阵 M，算法在 n^{O(√(n/ε))} 时间内找到值为 1-ε 的解，并在 n^{O(√q)} 时间内找到值为 q/n 的解。相比 Barak-Kothari-Steurer 的 exp(√n (log n)^{O(1)} / ε²) 和 Bhattiprolu 等人的 n^{O(q)}，运行时间有显著改进。

Conclusion: 本文通过简化舍入方法获得了更优的算法复杂度，同时新的 pinning lemma 变体具有独立的理论价值，可能对更广泛的舍入问题和高维概率研究有借鉴意义。

Abstract: We study the best separable state problem (BSS), which asks for the maximum acceptance probability of a quantum measurement over unentangled states. In classical terms, the goal is to maximize $\langle(x \otimes y), M (x \otimes y)\rangle$ over unit vectors $x,y$ where $0 \preceq M \preceq I$; we call this value $\mathrm{BSS}(M)$. We study $\mathrm{BSS}$ in the "perfect completeness" regime, where given $M$ such that $\mathrm{BSS}(M) = 1$ the goal is to find the best possible solution $x,y$ -- this generalizes the problem of finding a rank-one matrix as close as possible to a given subspace of $\mathbb{R}^{n \times n}$ guaranteed to contain a rank-one matrix. The strongest known algorithmic guarantees for this problem are: (1) an algorithm which finds a solution with value $1-\varepsilon$ in time $\exp(\sqrt{n} (\log n)^{O(1)} / \varepsilon^2)$, due to Barak, Kothari, and Steurer, and (2) an algorithm which finds a solution with value $q/n$ in time roughly $n^{O(q)}$, due to Bhattiprolu, Ghosh, Guruswami, Lee, and Tulsiani. We give a much simpler approach to rounding the SoS relaxation, generalizing the canonical "global correlation rounding" technique, and obtain a better running time. Given $M$ with $\mathrm{BSS}(M) = 1$, our algorithm finds a solution with value $1-ε$ in time $n^{O(\sqrt{n/\varepsilon})}$, and a solution of value $q/n$ in time $n^{O(\sqrt q)}$. Using the same techniques, we prove a new variant of the "pinning lemma", a measure-decomposition theorem widely used in LP/SDP rounding, high-dimensional probability, and statistical physics, which we believe is of independent interest.

</details>

Abstract: Crystal anisotropy provides a powerful route for realizing direction-dependent transport in solid-state systems. While its influence on electronic transport is well established, the role of anisotropy in magnon spin transport in van der Waals magnets is largely unexplored. Here, in a nonlocal geometry, utilizing the monoclinic van der Waals antiferromagnet CrPS$_4$, we observe pronounced anisotropy in both electrically and in thermally excited magnon spin transport. Electrically generated magnons exhibit a magnon spin conductivity at least 2.2 times larger and a spin diffusion length at least 2.7 times longer for transport along the crystallographic-b axis compared to the crystallographic-a axis, where $λ_m^{a} \sim$ 211 nm and $λ_m^{b} \geq$ 575 nm. In comparison, at 8T, we find the nonlocal second-harmonic resistance associated with thermally excited magnons to be $\sim$7 times larger along the crystallographic-b axis at 25K. We further show that a magnon spin diffusion length cannot be reliably extracted from the nonlocal second-harmonic resistance, owing to the extended temperature profile within CrPS$_4$. Likewise, we show that the anisotropy in the spin Seebeck coefficients cannot be reliably estimated from the thermally excited magnon spin transport alone, as it is intertwined with the anisotropic heat conductivity of CrPS$_4$. Utilizing the electrically generated magnon spin transport, we demonstrate that intrinsic crystalline anisotropy serves as an effective control parameter for tuning magnon spin transport, opening new avenues for magnonic device engineering.

Abstract: Although considered a niche technique, muon spectroscopy provides a unique and complementary insight into a range of different materials from hard condensed matter to biological samples and everything in between. In matter, the muon has a mass of $\frac{1}{9}~m_p$ or $207~m_e$, and is a local probe of quantum states that can provide a focus on the bulk properties of materials. While often interpreted in a classical framework, the muon is itself a quantum particle and it is increasingly common for researchers to take account of this when thinking about muon spectroscopy experiments. In this perspective, we focus on the power of using this quantum treatment of muon spectroscopy, which is a key future direction for the technique.

Abstract: Quantum software patterns provide high-level abstractions for building quantum programs, but there is still little empirical evidence on how they are adopted in practice. In prior work, we extended an existing quantum-pattern atlas into a 61-pattern catalog, created a knowledge base that links framework components to those patterns, and built a tool that mines pattern implementations from open-source code. We applied this tool on 80 projects and find that all 23 patterns occur in practice. In this work, we extend the tool with two additional matching channels and a vocabulary expansion step, and execute a quantitative evaluation of its accuracy on Qrisp, a framework not present in the knowledge base, reaching a micro-F1 of 0.712 against 0.449 without the expansion step. We then construct composition graphs that record calls among the high-level framework components associated with patterns and store them in a graph database. We use these graphs to examine how pattern implementations are assembled inside each framework, why patterns co-occur, and how much of a pattern's detection count comes from components called directly by developers rather than introduced through internal framework calls. We release qpa, an open-source mining pipeline, together with the knowledge base, which maps 286 framework components across five sources to the pattern catalog, maintained with the support of an LLM ensemble that classifies newly extracted components, and the resulting pattern usage dataset, to support reproducible studies on the adoption and evolution of quantum patterns.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 量子局部可恢复码（qLRCs）在量子容错计算和分布式量子存储中具有重要意义，能够在部分量子位故障时通过少量量子位实现高效恢复。现有的qLRC研究多聚焦于简单局部性，而经典的(r,t,x)-LRC引入了多个恢复集和交参数的概念，能提供更灵活的恢复策略和更高的容错能力。本文旨在将经典(r,t,x)-LRC理论推广到量子领域，建立CSS构造下的等价关系，并构造具有良好参数的二元qLRC码族。

Result: 论文的主要结果包括：建立了CSS码与经典(r,t,x)-LRC之间的充要条件等价关系；利用子集包含矩阵成功构造了二元双包含(r,t,x)-cLRC族，进而通过CSS构造得到二元(r,t,x)-qLRC；获得了CSS (r,t,x)-qLRC的维数和速率上界、纯情况下的最小距离上界以及精确情况下的Singleton类维数上界；所构造的码族被证明能达到高速率且具有非平凡的最小距离。

Abstract: In this work, we study $(r,t,x)$ quantum locally recoverable codes (qLRCs) with locality $r$, $t$ recovery sets per qudit, and intersection parameter $x$. We first show that, assuming the underlying classical codes have dual minimum distance at least two, a CSS code is an $(r,t,x)$-qLRC if and only if the underlying classical codes are $(r,t,x)$ classical LRCs (cLRCs) with common recovery sets. We then use subset-inclusion matrices to construct families of binary dual-containing $(r,t,x)$-cLRCs, which yield binary $(r,t,x)$-qLRCs via the CSS construction. For CSS $(r,t,x)$-qLRCs, we derive upper bounds on the dimension and rate, minimum-distance bounds in the pure case, and a Singleton-like dimension bound in the exact case. Abstract: We study pure disjoint $(r,δ)$-quantum locally recoverable codes (qLRCs) without assuming a stabilizer structure. Abstract: In semiconductor single-spin qubits, high-fidelity quantum gates have been demonstrated; however, achieving consistent performance remains challenging due to variations in driven qubit coherence, which is less explored than free-evolution coherence such as $T_2^*$. Here, we report single-qubit gate fidelities above 99.999%, achieved by dramatically extending the driven-spin coherence time and suppressing off-resonant driving effects that are detrimental to accurate fidelity benchmarking. We demonstrate that removing proximal reservoirs significantly enhances the spin-locking coherence time ($T_{1ρ}$), a critical metric for qubits under microwave driving. Furthermore, we reveal that in typical spin qubit setups using parity readout and rectangular pulses, off-resonant excitation of neighboring qubits causes substantial benchmarking artifacts. By optimizing device conditions to mitigate microwave-induced degradation and implementing spectrally tailored pulse shaping, we achieve a $π/2$ gate fidelity of 99.99920(2)%, with remaining errors primarily limited by incoherent noise. These results showcase the mechanisms that bound fidelity benchmarking in state-of-the-art silicon spin qubits and provide practical guidelines for achieving and verifying high fidelities in these systems.

### [65] [Equilibrium Distributions for Strongly Nonlinear Many-Body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10352)
*Jialin Zhang,Yong Zhang,Hong Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于广义能量均分原理的框架，用于确定非线性系统的平衡分布。该框架在弱非线性极限下恢复现有修正，更重要的是在强非线性条件下仍然有效，此时扰动贡献相互关联而传统方法失效。通过在非线性薛定谔方程、Majda-McLaughlin-Tabak模型和Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou模型上的数值模拟，验证了该方法可处理的非线性强度比传统理论高出一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性系统的平衡分布对于精确计算宏观可观测量至关重要，但传统的理论修正通常仅限于弱非线性情形，其假设相互作用项可以视为非相关的微扰（即随机相位近似成立）。当非线性增强时，微扰贡献变得相互关联，传统方法失效，亟需一种适用于强非线性的新框架。

Method: 作者基于广义能量均分原理（generalized energy equipartition principle）发展了一套确定平衡分布的框架。该框架在弱非线性区域能恢复已有的修正结果，同时通过更一般化的处理方式，使其在强非线性下（微扰贡献相互关联时）仍然有效。在三个代表性非线性模型（NLS方程、MMT模型、FPUT模型）上进行了数值验证。

Result: 数值模拟表明，该框架在三个非线性模型上均能提供准确的修正，可处理的非线性强度比传统理论所能适用的范围强出一个数量级以上（more than an order of magnitude stronger）。

Conclusion: 该工作突破了传统弱非线性微扰理论的局限，基于广义能量均分原理为强非线性系统的平衡分布提供了统一且有效的理论框架，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Obtaining equilibrium distributions of nonlinear systems is essential for accurately computing macroscopic observables. Conventional theoretical corrections are typically limited to weak nonlinearities, where interaction terms can be treated as effectively uncorrelated perturbations and the random phase approximation applies. In this Letter, we develop a framework to determine equilibrium distributions based on the generalized energy equipartition principle. Our approach recovers existing corrections in the weakly nonlinear regime and, crucially, remains valid for strong nonlinearities, where perturbative contributions become correlated and conventional approaches break down. Numerical simulations of the nonlinear Schrödinger equation, the Majda-McLaughlin-Tabak model, and the Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou model demonstrate accurate corrections for nonlinearities more than an order of magnitude stronger than those accessible to conventional theories.

</details>


### [66] [Anomalous current fluctuations in the stochastic XNOR hopping model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10536)
*Balázs Pozsgay*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机XNOR过程中自旋电流的涨落，提出了三个关于长时间电流分布的猜想：非零磁化同质初始态下t^{1/4}尺度的高斯极限、相反磁化畴壁态的t^{1/4}尺度半正态极限、以及零磁化同质初始态的t^{1/8}尺度M-Wright极限。作者从微观XNOR动力学出发预测了连续时间过程的缺失振幅，并将其推广到同质偏置初始数据。所有猜想均通过无拟合参数的数值模拟得到支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动力学受限的随机XNOR跳变模型中，自旋电流的长时间涨落行为尚未被完整刻画。此前研究已识别出示踪粒子机制并预期了畴壁态和零磁化情形下的标度指数与极限形状，但缺少振幅的具体数值预测，也尚未推广到同质偏置初始态。

Method: 基于微观XNOR随机动力学，推导连续时间过程中各猜想所对应的涨落振幅；针对三类初始条件（非零磁化同质、相反磁化畴壁、零磁化同质）分别提出具有明确振幅的极限分布猜想，并通过数值模拟进行无拟合参数验证。

Result: 提出了三个猜想：非零磁化同质初始态在t^{1/4}尺度的高斯极限、畴壁态在t^{1/4}尺度的半正态极限、零磁化同质初始态在t^{1/8}尺度的M-Wright极限，并给出了这些极限的显式振幅。数值模拟在没有拟合参数的情况下支持了所有三个猜想。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了随机XNOR过程在多种初始条件下的自旋电流长期涨落行为，填补了振幅预测的空白并将结果推广至更一般的初始条件。数学证明由配套论文以AI生成的候选证明形式提供。

Abstract: We consider fluctuations of the spin current in the stochastic XNOR process, a kinetically constrained hopping model in one spatial dimension. We formulate three conjectures with explicit amplitudes for the long-time current distributions: a Gaussian limit on the $t^{1/4}$ scale for homogeneous initial states with nonzero magnetization, a half-normal limit on the $t^{1/4}$ scale for domain-wall states with opposite magnetizations, and an M-Wright limit on the $t^{1/8}$ scale for homogeneous initial states at zero magnetization. Earlier work identified the tracer mechanism and anticipated the scaling exponents and limiting shapes in the domain-wall and zero-magnetization settings. Starting from the microscopic XNOR dynamics, we predict the missing amplitudes for the continuous-time process and extend the picture to homogeneous biased initial data. Simulations provide numerical support for all three conjectures without fitted parameters. A separate mathematical companion paper presents an extensively AI-generated candidate proof. Generative-AI tools were used in the research and writing workflow.

</details>


### [67] [Steady-state phase transition in one-dimensional hybrid contact process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10948)
*Lin Shang,Shuai Geng,Xingli Li,Jiasen Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维混合接触过程（hybrid contact process）的稳态相变。通过基于有效场的单点和团簇平均场近似，找到了系统所有可能的稳态，揭示了稳定吸收相、活性相以及双稳区域的存在。在吸收相与双稳区域边界观察到鞍结分岔，表明该处为不连续相变；而吸收相到活性相则为连续相变。利用相干异常方法提取了连续相变的真实临界点和临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合接触过程是理解非平衡相变和非经典临界行为的重要模型，但其稳态相图及相变性质（连续/不连续）尚不清晰，需要系统的平均场分析来阐明不同稳态区域的结构及临界标度行为。

Method: 采用单点和团簇平均场近似（基于有效场），求解系统所有可能的稳态；通过分析稳态稳定性确定吸收相、活性相及双稳区域；利用鞍结分岔识别不连续相变边界；采用相干异常方法（Coherent Anomaly Method, CAM）从近似结果中提取精确临界点和临界指数。

Result: 发现系统存在稳定的吸收相和活性相，以及在长时间极限下的双稳区域。吸收相与双稳区之间存在鞍结分岔，对应不连续相变；吸收相到活性相的转变是连续的。通过相干异常方法获得了连续相变的非经典临界指数和真实临界点，刻画了其非经典标度行为。

Conclusion: 混合接触过程在稳态下表现出丰富的相图结构，兼具连续和不连续相变特征，其中连续相变呈现非经典（非平均场）临界标度，可通过相干异常方法有效提取其精确临界性质。

Abstract: We investigate the steady-state phase transition in a one-dimensional hybrid contact process. We implement the single-site and cluster mean-field approximations based on the effective fields and present all the possible steady states of the system. We show the existence of the stable absorbing and active phases, and the bistable region in the long-time limit. The saddle-node bifurcation is observed at the boundary between the absorbing phase and the bistable region, suggesting a discontinuous phase transition. While the absorbing to active phase transition is continuous. To characterize the nonclassical scaling behavior of the continuous phase transition, we extract the true critical points and exponents by means of the coherent anomaly method.

</details>


### [68] [Jamming transition in an active exclusion process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11041)
*Kavita Jain,Sakuntala Chatterjee*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维活性粒子系统中活性（self-propulsion）对阻塞相变（jamming transition）的影响。通过蒙特卡洛模拟和平均场理论，发现活性会阻碍甚至抑制阻塞相的形成：临界密度随开关速率的减小而降低，当开关速率足够小时，系统只存在流体相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究表明活性可以诱导或抑制相变，或改变被动系统的临界行为，但活性如何影响阻塞相变这一非平衡相变的典型范式尚未被系统研究。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 考虑一维硬核相互作用活性粒子系统，自推进方向以给定开关速率反转。对于一类粒子跳跃速率且开关速率无穷大的情况，模型退化为已知具有高密度流体相与低密度阻塞相之间相变的被动系统。采用蒙特卡洛模拟和平均场理论，研究平均粒子迁移率和空穴团簇分布随密度和有限开关速率的变化。

Result: 主要发现为活性阻碍阻塞的形成甚至可完全抑制其发生：阻塞相变发生在随开关速率减小而降低的临界密度处；当开关速率足够小时，系统仅存在于流体相。

Conclusion: 活性通过阻碍空穴团簇的形成与聚集，抑制了阻塞相的建立，从而改变了阻塞相变的临界行为，表明活性粒子的非平衡特性可显著调控阻塞这一典型的非平衡相变。

Abstract: Multiple studies on active matter have shown that activity can induce or suppress a phase transition, or modify the critical behavior of a passive system. Here we investigate how activity affects the jamming transition which is a paradigmatic example of nonequilibrium phase transitions in passive systems. We consider a one-dimensional system of active particles with hardcore interactions and a direction of self-propulsion which can be reversed at a given switching rate. For a class of particle hop rates and infinite switching rate, our model reduces to a passive system which is known to exhibit a transition between a high-density fluid phase and a low-density jammed phase in the stationary state. Using Monte Carlo simulations and a mean field theory, we study how the mean mobility of the particle and the hole cluster distribution vary with density and finite switching rate. Our main result is that activity hinders the formation of jam and can even inhibit it; more precisely, we find that the jamming transition occurs at a critical density that decreases with decreasing switching rate, and at sufficiently small switching rate, the system exists only in the fluid phase.

</details>


### [69] [Exact Expressions of Entropy for Classical Non-interacting Many-body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11104)
*Yuheng Wu,Henrik Heelweg,Rigel Galgana*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"平稳性"和"无偏性"两个公设的普适系综理论，为任意大小的经典无相互作用多体系统在所有统计系综中提供精确的熵表达式，并证明不同系综的熵在大系统极限下收敛到与大型偏差理论一致的通用渐近形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在热力学极限下，所有统计系综的热力学性质是等价的；但对于有限尺寸系统，系综等价性不再精确成立，不同系综给出的热力学量可能显著不同。作者希望发展一种适用于任意尺寸系统的普适系综理论，以严格量化这些有限尺寸效应并给出精确的熵表达式。

Method: 基于仅两个公设建立理论：(1) 平稳性——物理定律在时间平移下不变；(2) 无偏性——平衡混合态在给定约束条件下使熵最大化。由此推导出所有统计系综中经典无相互作用多体系统的精确熵表达式。

Result: 获得了各系综中的精确熵表达式，并证明这些表达式在热力学极限下收敛到统一的渐近形式 S ≈ ln((4πm e E/3N)^(3N/2)·(V/N)^N) + N，与大型偏差理论的预言一致。

Conclusion: 该理论为任意尺寸的多体系统提供了严格的系综描述框架，弥合了有限尺寸系统与热力学极限之间的鸿沟，且所得渐近形式验证了与大型偏差理论的一致性。

Abstract: In the thermodynamic limit, the equilibrium state of a many-body system can be characterized by three pairs of conjugate thermodynamic variables: $E/T,V/P,N/μ$. In this limit, the thermodynamic properties in all ensembles are equivalent up to the leading order of $E,V,N$. However, for systems of finite size, this ensemble equivalence is no longer exact, and the thermodynamic properties may differ substantially among ensembles. To quantify these finite-size effects rigorously, it is desirable to develop a universal ensemble theory applicable to systems of arbitrary size, providing exact expressions for entropy and, thereby, giving rise to the precise value of all equilibrium thermodynamic quantities. In this work, we propose a theory that determines the exact entropy expressions for classical non-interacting many-body systems of arbitrary size across all statistical ensembles, based on only two postulates: \textbf{stationarity}, requiring that the physical laws be invariant under time translation, and \textbf{unbiasedness}, requiring that the equilibrium mixed state maximize the entropy subject to the prescribed constraints. Moreover, we show that the entropy expressions obtained in different ensembles converge to the common asymptotic form $S \asymp \ln\!\left( \left( \frac{4πm e E}{3N} \right)^{3N/2} \cdot \left(\frac{V}{N}\right)^N \right)+N$, consistent with the predictions of the large deviation theory.

</details>


### [70] [Exact First-Passage Time Response Theory from Steady-State Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11202)
*Ruicheng Bao,Shiling Liang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个紧凑的理论框架，用于研究连续时间马尔可夫过程中平均首达时间（MFPT）对扰动的线性和非线性响应。核心工具是一个精确对应关系，将MFPT的瞬态响应映射到辅助系统的稳态响应，从而得到完全由未扰动MFPT和稳态概率表达的通用响应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MFPT是物理、化学和生物系统中传输、反应、搜索和切换过程的普适时间度量，理解其对扰动的响应对于预测和控制至关重要，但此前缺乏系统性的理论框架。

Method: 建立一个精确对应关系，将MFPT（瞬态响应）映射到辅助系统的稳态响应，从而导出任意状态对之间MFPT响应关系的精确通用公式。进一步将MFPT响应分解为线性上游、线性下游和非线性三部分。基于此框架推导出响应曲线推断规则、MFPT响应的基本界、高阶响应解析表达式以及多速率响应公式。

Result: 获得了完全由未扰动MFPT和稳态概率表达的通用响应关系；得到了MFPT响应的因子化物理分解；推导出响应曲线推断规则、MFPT响应的基本界、高阶响应表达式和多速率响应公式。该方法在计算MFPT和稳态分布方面具有计算优势。此外，分析了生物学相关的折叠网络，并澄清了近期报道的MFPT悖论。

Conclusion: 该框架为MFPT对扰动的响应提供了系统、精确且通用的理论工具，兼具理论洞见和计算实用性，并成功应用于解释生物折叠网络中的现象。

Abstract: The mean first-passage time (MFPT) provides a universal temporal measure of transport, reaction, search, and switching processes in physical, chemical, and biological systems. Understanding how MFPTs respond to perturbations is therefore crucial for prediction and control, yet a systematic theory has been lacking. We establish a compact theoretical framework for linear and nonlinear MFPT response in continuous-time Markov processes. The key tool is an exact correspondence that maps the intrinsically transient response of MFPTs onto the steady-state response of an auxiliary system. This correspondence yields exact and universal response relations for MFPTs between arbitrary state pairs, expressed entirely in terms of unperturbed MFPTs and steady-state probabilities. We then obtain a factorized physical decomposition of the MFPT response into linear upstream, linear downstream, and nonlinear contributions. Further corollaries include response-curve inference rules, fundamental bounds on MFPT responses, analytical expressions for higher-order responses of MFPTs and steady-state probabilities, and multi-rate response formulas. Additionally, our result offers computational advantages in calculating both MFPTs and steady-state distributions. Finally, a biologically motivated folding network is analyzed, and a recently reported paradox on MFPT is clarified.

</details>


### [71] [Bounds for Apparent Second-Law Violations in Quantum Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10118)
*Domingos S. P. Salazar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究量子轨迹动力学中负随机熵产生的物理意义，通过引入动力学不对称项σ*完成Ω=σ+σ*，证明似然比符号是最优的反转奇轨迹可观测量，并建立了一个尖锐的涨落定理下限，适用于修正平局后的物理符号统计量Π_σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负随机熵产生常被视为对第二定律的表观违反，但一般量子轨迹动力学中物理熵产生σ不满足前向详细涨落定理。作者希望澄清这一悖论，并为负熵产生事件的频率建立严格定量界限。

Method: 通过任意耦合的通用形式化方法，识别出动力学不对称项σ*完成Ω=σ+σ*。证明似然比符号在反转奇轨迹可观测量中是最优的，并将其用于将尖锐涨落定理下限迁移到修正平局后的物理符号统计量。给出了显式的测量记录协议，并在随机有限耦合碰撞模型和相干驱动量子比特与热辅助系统相互作用中进行数值验证。

Result: 当Pr(σ=0)=0时，得到Pr(σ<0)≥[1-⟨Ω⟩/g(⟨Ω⟩)]/2的严格下限，其中g是a↦a tanh(a/2)的逆函数。物理积分涨落定理同时抑制大的负事件，产生定量化的"频繁但温和"定律，而符号不平衡为隐藏均值Σ*提供了下限。

Conclusion: 负熵产生并不构成对第二定律的真正违反，而是可以通过动力学不对称校正后得到严格的涨落定理约束。该方法提供了负熵事件频率和规模的定量界限，并揭示了物理符号统计量与隐藏动力学不对称项之间的联系。

Abstract: Negative stochastic entropy production is commonly called an apparent violation of the second law. In general quantum-trajectory dynamics, however, the physical entropy production $σ$ need not obey a forward detailed fluctuation theorem. A general arbitrary-coupling formulation identifies a dynamical-asymmetry term $σ^\ast$ that completes it into $Ω=σ+σ^\ast$, whose mean is $\langleΩ\rangle=Σ+Σ^\ast$. We prove that the likelihood-ratio sign is optimal among reversal-odd trajectory observables and use this fact to transfer an established sharp fluctuation-theorem floor to the tie-corrected physical sign statistic $Π_σ=\Pr(σ<0)+\Pr(σ=0)/2$. When $\Pr(σ=0)=0$, the result reads $\Pr(σ<0)\ge[1-\langleΩ\rangle/g(\langleΩ\rangle)]/2$, where $g$ is the inverse of $a\mapsto a\tanh(a/2)$. The physical integral fluctuation theorem simultaneously suppresses large negative events, producing a quantitative ``frequent but mild'' law, while the sign imbalance lower-bounds the hidden mean $Σ^\ast$. We formulate the measured-record protocol explicitly and illustrate and numerically audit the tie-corrected theorem in random finite-coupling collision models and a coherently driven qubit interacting with thermal ancillas.

</details>


### [72] [Universal scaling of spatially extended zero modes in inhomogeneous SSH chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11021)
*Gilles Parez,Nicolas Crampé,Quentin Labriet,Lucia Morey,Luc Vinet*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了非均匀Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）模型中光滑界面对拓扑零模的影响。通过精确晶格解与非均匀Dirac描述的相结合，发现Jackiw-Rebbi零模在光滑界面下会空间延展，其延展尺度与系统尺寸的平方根成正比，与界面微观细节无关。该涌现长度定义了介于两个带隙相之间的介观临界区域，在此区域内关联呈现代数衰减后过渡到指数衰减，纠缠熵则按该长度的对数标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑相的标志性特征是受保护的零模在尖锐界面处呈指数局域化。然而，实际物理系统中界面往往是光滑的，需要理解光滑界面如何改变这一拓扑保护的基本图景。

Method: 结合了精确晶格求解与非均匀Dirac方程描述两种方法，对一大类非均匀SSH模型进行解析分析，推导光滑跳跃轮廓下零模的扩展行为及其普适标度律。

Result: 发现光滑界面的零模扩展长度与系统尺寸的平方根成正比，该标度与界面微观细节无关；在此介观临界区域内关联函数呈代数衰减，纠缠熵随扩展长度的对数增长。

Conclusion: 确立了控制光滑拓扑界面低能物理的普适临界长度，揭示了光滑界面下拓扑零模从指数局域化到代数延展的新奇行为，拓展了对拓扑相变中临界区域的理解。

Abstract: Protected zero modes are a hallmark of topological phases of matter and are exponentially localized at sharp interfaces between distinct gapped phases. We investigate how this picture changes for smooth interfaces in a broad class of inhomogeneous Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) models. This emergent length defines a mesoscopic critical region separating two gapped phases, within which correlations decay algebraically before crossing over to exponential decay. In addition, the entanglement entropy scales as the logarithm of the emergent length near the interface, confirming the interpretation of a mesoscopic critical region. Paradigmatic examples are the spin-boson and Anderson impurity models, in which system degrees of freedom interact with either bosonic or fermionic gapless baths. Capturing the full continuum of many-body excitations in the WDF requires a non-perturbative solution of the underlying non-equilibrium quantum impurity problem. Abstract: Modern data science increasingly gives rise to hypothesis-testing problems that are not naturally formulated in terms of parameters within prespecified statistical models. One important example is the dynamic evaluation of optimization algorithms, where decisions must be made during training about whether further updates remain beneficial or the algorithm should switch to a different phase. This issue is particularly relevant in stochastic min-max optimization. Generative adversarial networks (GANs) provide a canonical example, as their training requires repeated decisions about when to switch between discriminator and generator updates, yet existing methods typically rely on fixed update ratios or heuristic criteria. We formulate this switching problem as sequential hypothesis testing and develop an e-process-based adaptive training procedure. During discriminator updates, one e-process tests the null that the discriminator-induced separation between the empirical data distribution and the generator law remains below a target level. During generator updates, with the discriminator fixed, a second e-process tests the reverse null that this separation remains above a refresh level. Conditional on the observed training sample, we prove that fresh empirical indices and latent draws yield conditional e-values that can be accumulated into e-processes, providing anytime-valid Type I error control under adaptive model updates and data-dependent switching. Across multimodal synthetic distributions and image benchmark datasets, the proposed method matches or outperforms the best fixed-ratio baselines under several widely used GAN objectives.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者采用多重方法：(1) 在高斯总体构造中，证明可逆剪切变换保持预测问题和三个模型定义量，却将累积欧氏擦除计数从一变为二，此结论对Moore-Penrose OLS和所有有限非负ridge权重成立；(2) 针对双输出全QR过程，展示累积编辑秩从二变为环境维度四；(3) 证明完整累积度量QR轨迹在正定度量、探针、正则化器及tie-breaking一致迁移时具有仿射等变性；(4) 在已知秩有限样本Adam/QR校准中，对比恒等混合与不同剪切参数下的接受更新次数；(5) 对冻结V-JEPA2特征进行受控重参数化实验，保持秩零预测的同时改变欧氏轨迹。

Result: 研究表明：累积欧氏擦除计数和累积编辑秩都可在信息保持的可逆重参数化下改变；模型定义量与程序定义量有本质区分；精确协方差是仿射等边性的推论而非规范语义度量；在Adam/QR校准中，恒等混合20次大样本运行均只接受一次更新，而所有测试剪切参数均接受至少两次更新；冻结V-JEPA2特征的重参数化保持预测不变但改变后续欧氏轨迹。

Conclusion: 迭代擦除返回的是由表示几何和完整测量程序共同决定的过程相对估计量，不是独立于测量程序的语义维度。因此，基于擦除计数来报告"概念维度"的方法需要重新审视，应区分模型定义的总体量与程序定义量，并谨慎对待任何脱离测量程序的维度声明。

Abstract: How many directions does a neural representation use to encode a concept? A common answer repeatedly erases probe directions and reports the stopping count or cumulative removed rank. We show that both quantities can change under an information-preserving invertible reparameterization, so neither is intrinsically a concept dimension. We distinguish model-defined population quantities (generating dimension, sufficient linear dimension, and minimum guarding rank) from procedure-defined quantities such as stopping count and cumulative edit rank. In a population Gaussian construction, an invertible shear preserves the prediction problem and all three quantities, yet changes the cumulative Euclidean erasure count from one to two. The separation holds for Moore--Penrose ordinary least squares and every finite nonnegative ridge weight. For a two-output full-QR procedure matching our motivating video analysis, cumulative edit rank similarly changes from two to the ambient dimension four. Conversely, the complete cumulative metric-QR trajectory is affine-equivariant when its positive-definite metric, probe, regularizer, and tie-breaking are transported consistently; exact covariance is one corollary, not a canonical semantic metric. In a known-rank finite-sample Adam/QR calibration, identity mixing stops after one accepted update in all 20 large-sample runs, whereas each tested shear $a\in\{.5,.75,1,1.25,2\}$ accepts at least two updates in all 20 runs. Controlled reparameterizations of frozen V-JEPA2 features preserve rank-zero predictions yet alter later Euclidean trajectories under practical optimization. These visual contact experiments are stress tests, not estimates of contact dimension. Iterative erasure therefore returns a procedure-relative estimand jointly determined by representation geometry and the full measurement procedure, not a semantic dimension by itself.

Abstract: Spectral clustering methods for network data are commonly based on a few matrix representations, such as the adjacency matrix and the symmetric Laplacian. We study a continuum of degree-normalized spectral embeddings that includes these commonly used choices as special cases. Abstract: Constant-stepsize temporal-difference (TD) learning is attractive for policy evaluation, but inference from a single Markov trajectory must account for serial dependence and a stepsize-dependent stationary target. For such a contrast, the procedure admits a one-pass implementation whose memory does not grow with the trajectory length. At a fixed stepsize, the inferential center is the RR stationary target. We also study horizon-indexed designs in which the stepsize remains constant within each run and decreases across longer horizons. Under an explicit RR-dependent rate window, the residual RR target shift, multiplicative remainder, and initialization effect are negligible at the root-$n$ scale, yielding inference for the projected Bellman solution. Experiments on FrozenLake and Garnet illustrate stationary-target coverage, RR target correction, and the finite-sample behavior of the horizon-indexed design.
