TL;DR: 本文系统综述了条件独立性（CI）检验方法，重点探讨其在基于约束的因果发现（如PC和FCI算法）中的核心作用，将CI测试方法归为六大类（偏相关、列联表、回归、近邻、核和机器学习方法），并深入分析各类方法的假设条件、鲁棒性和高维混合数据场景下的可扩展性，同时将测试层面的性质（如功效随条件集大小衰减、I型/II型错误的不对称后果）与图层面的错误（骨架恢复和v-结构定向）联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI测试是约束式因果发现的统计引擎，但现有文献缺乏一个系统性的综览来梳理不同CI方法族的假设、局限与鲁棒性方案，特别是在生物医学领域常见的高维和混合类型数据场景下，如何选择适当的CI测试方法并理解其失效模式仍是一个未解决的挑战。

Method: 该综述将CI测试方法系统性地组织为六个家族：偏相关、列联表、回归、最近邻、核方法以及基于机器学习的测试。对每个家族，作者考察其CI决策何时能反映数据生成分布、何时会失效，并特别强调应对各方法局限性的鲁棒性层。综述还跨主流R和Python库比较了各方法的采用情况，并将测试层面的统计性质与图层面的因果发现错误相连接。

Result: 综述建立了CI测试层面属性（条件集大小导致的功效衰减、不对称的I型/II型错误后果）与图层面错误（骨架恢复错误和v-结构定向错误）之间的关联，总结了不同方法家族在R和Python主流库中的采用情况，并识别了开放挑战：无需离散化的混合类型CI测试、小样本误差控制，以及提升CI测试可扩展性的策略。

Conclusion: 作者指出尽管CI测试方法已十分丰富，但在混合类型数据、小样本和高维场景下仍存在显著的未解决问题，需要更有效的鲁棒性策略和可扩展方案来支撑生物医学领域的因果发现实践。

Abstract: Conditional Independence (CI) tests are the statistical engine of constraint-based causal discovery: in algorithms such as PC (Peter-Clark) and FCI (Fast Causal Inference), skeleton pruning and key orientations follow directly from CI decisions. This survey reviews CI testing with emphasis on assumptions, robustness, and scalability in high-dimensional and mixed-type settings common in biomedical domains. The survey organizes widely used CI methods into six families: partial-correlation, contingency-table, regression, nearest-neighbor, kernel, and machine-learning-based. Special emphasis is provided on the robustness layers that address the limitations of these families. For each family, the survey examines when CI decisions reflect the data-generating distribution and when they fail. By this, we link test-level properties, including power decay with conditioning set size and asymmetric type I/II error consequences, to graph-level errors in skeleton recovery and v-structure orientation. The survey also compares adoption across major R and Python libraries and summarizes open challenges, including mixed-type CI testing without discretization, small-sample error control, and strategies for improving scalability of CI-testing.

</details>


### [80] [Optimized Sequential Testing for Binary Ensemble Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15237)
*Joseph Kalman,Amit Moscovich*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出利用序贯检验（sequential testing）思想来降低集成分类器（如随机森林）的计算成本：不再评估所有基模型并投票，而是顺序评估基模型，一旦出现明显多数即提前停止。作者定义了三种最优性概念来设计提前停止策略，通过线性规划高效求解，在控制与完整集成不一致率（如0.1%）的前提下，在真实数据集上实现了4倍以上的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成分类器（如随机森林）通过组合多个基模型获得高精度，但基模型数量越多，训练和推理成本越高。现有的集成方法通常需要评估所有基模型才做出预测，造成不必要的计算开销。作者希望在不显著损失预测准确性的前提下降低集成的计算成本。

Method: 采用序贯检验的思想：按顺序评估基模型的预测结果，当明显多数出现时提前停止，无需评估全部基模型。作者定义了三种不同的最优性准则（用于最小化执行的基模型数量，同时控制与完整集成的分歧率），并对每种准则和允许的分歧率构造可高效求解的线性规划，以求得最优停止策略。

Result: 在UC Irvine机器学习库的真实数据集以及Grinsztajn等人提出的基准数据集上进行了测试。结果表明在大多数数据集上，这些方法在将分歧率控制在0.1%的前提下，实现了4倍或更高的加速比。

Conclusion: 基于序贯检验的提前停止策略能够在保证与完整集成预测几乎一致（分歧率极低）的同时，显著降低集成分类器的推理计算成本，为高效集成推理提供了可行且可优化的理论框架。

Abstract: Ensemble classifiers are predictive models that combine the results of simpler base models, often by majority vote. A classic example is random forests, which combine the predictions of decision trees. Ensembles that use more base models can be more accurate but also more costly to train and run. In this paper, we consider strategies for reducing the computational cost of binary classification using an approach from the field of sequential testing. Rather than evaluating all the base models and taking a majority vote, we evaluate the base models sequentially and stop execution when a clear majority emerges. We consider three different notions of optimality for early-stopping strategies that minimize the number of base models executed while controlling the rate of disagreement with the full ensemble. For each notion of optimality and allowable disagreement rate, we show that a linear program can be constructed and solved efficiently to find the optimal stopping strategy. We tested these methods on real-world datasets taken from the UC Irvine Machine Learning repository, and on the benchmark datasets proposed by Grinsztajn et al. We found that on most datasets, these methods provide speed-ups of 4x or more while controlling disagreement at 0.1%

</details>


### [81] [Optimal Inference with Black-box Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10155)
*Lucas Kania,Abhinav Chakraborty,Edward Kennedy,Larry Wasserman,Sivaraman Balakrishnan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在高维高斯序列模型下，刻画了结合黑盒预测进行统计推断的信息论极限，并开发了能够自适应未知预测精度且利用预测间强对齐性的实用假设检验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有许多工作提出将观测数据与预测相结合进行有效统计推断的方法，但该领域缺乏一个统一原理来解释假设检验应如何以既有效又高效的方式整合数据和预测。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 在高维高斯序列模型框架下，分别刻画了预测精度已知时以及正交预测且精度未知时的信息论推断极限。基于这些刻画结果，开发了能够自适应未知预测精度的实用假设检验方法。

Result: 建立了结合黑盒预测进行推断的信息论下界/极限特征，并提出了实际的检验方法，这些方法能适应预测精度的未知性，同时能从预测之间的强对齐性中获益。

Conclusion: 本文为解决"如何将数据与预测整合以实现有效且高效的统计推断"这一开放问题提供了统一的理论框架，在高维高斯序列模型下给出了信息论极限的完整刻画，并提供了实际可行的检验程序。

Abstract: Powerful black-box predictive models have motivated many proposals for combining observed data with predictions to perform valid statistical inference. Despite this progress, the field lacks a unifying principle that explains how hypothesis tests should integrate data and predictions in a way that is both valid and efficient. In this work, we address this gap in the high-dimensional Gaussian sequence model. We characterize the information-theoretic limits of inference with black-box predictions when their accuracies are known and, for orthogonal predictions, when they are unknown. Building on these characterizations, we develop practical hypothesis tests that adapt to the unknown accuracies of the predictions while benefiting from strong alignment among them.

</details>


### [82] [COMPACT: Spectral Adjustment Scores from a Complete and Irreducible Causal Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10305)
*Eric V. Strobl*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于广义特征值问题的因果调整变量选择方法，超越传统倾向得分的平衡评分框架，通过识别最大化特异性图形类别的得分空间，使得在存在潜在变量和弱混杂信息分布的情况下仍能有效进行因果效应估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测数据集通常包含许多基线变量，研究者往往不知道应在调整集中包含哪些变量，且混杂信息可能弱分布在多个变量上。倾向得分虽然可以将高维协变量压缩为标量，但其作为最粗平衡评分的分布最优性并不保证在因果图上的最大特异性。需要一种既能处理高维协变量、又能最大化因果图特异性的得分方法。

Method: 在忠实性假设下，作者考察候选得分、处理与结果之间的所有因果图（允许潜在变量），识别一组不受处理是否导致结果影响的最大无条件与条件依赖关系集合，从而定义可通过这些关系表达的最大特异性图形类别。提出的算法通过广义特征值问题求解，其得分空间同时目标于平衡坐标和结果引导坐标的张成空间；利用充分信息性的代理变量可在不直接观测调整变量的情况下恢复该张成空间。

Result: 作者刻画了相应的估计误差和因果误差，并建立了完整过程的bootstrap有效性。模拟实验和实际数据应用表明，该算法在多个备选方案中表现优越。

Conclusion: 该方法为因果效应估计中的调整变量选择提供了超越传统倾向得分的新框架，能够在高维、弱混杂信息分布和潜在变量的复杂场景下识别最优得分空间，从而实现更准确的因果估计。

Abstract: Observational datasets frequently contain many baseline variables, yet investigators estimating causal effects may not know which variables to include in the adjustment set. Confounding information may also be distributed weakly across many variables. Propensity scores can simplify adjustment by reducing high-dimensional covariates to a scalar with binary treatment. Although the propensity score is the coarsest balancing score, this distributional optimality does not imply maximal specificity over causal graphs. We instead examine all causal graphs among a candidate score, treatment, and outcome while allowing latent variables. Under faithfulness, we identify the largest set of unconditional and conditional dependence relations whose truth is invariant to whether treatment causes the outcome, leaving treatment-effect estimation to the downstream analysis. This criterion defines the maximally specific graph class expressible through these relations. We then develop the proposed algorithm, which operationalizes the criterion through a generalized eigenvalue problem whose score space targets the span of a balancing coordinate and an outcome-guided coordinate. We show that sufficiently informative proxies can recover this span without direct observation of the adjustment variables, characterize the resulting estimation and causal errors, and establish bootstrap validity for the complete procedure. Simulations and a real-data application demonstrate superior performance over several alternatives.

</details>


### [83] [Improved cross-validated distances for multivariate pattern analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10394)
*Laurent Caplette,Sarah Lippé*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对Guggenmos等人推荐的多元模式分析距离度量（交叉验证欧氏距离和类内校正皮尔逊距离）进行改进，提出了广义交叉验证距离和交叉验证相关距离，在可靠性和准确性上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guggenmos等人（2018）评估了多种用于衡量神经表征差异性的距离度量，推荐使用交叉验证欧氏距离或类内校正皮尔逊距离，但作者认为这些度量仍存在改进空间，希望进一步提升其可靠性和准确性。

Method: 首先证明交叉验证欧氏距离等价于各划分间距离之和，并利用这一等价关系推导出广义变体；其次基于欧氏距离与皮尔逊相关之间的关系，定义交叉验证相关距离；最后讨论广义交叉验证与类内校正两种策略之间的关系，并比较它们在相关距离上的准确性。

Result: 广义交叉验证变体在可靠性和准确性上均优于原始交叉验证欧氏距离；所提出的交叉验证相关距离比Guggenmos等人的表述更准确且更可解释；对于相关距离，广义交叉验证比类内校正能获得更高的准确性。

Conclusion: 广义交叉验证距离和交叉验证相关距离是对现有MEG多变量模式分析中距离度量的有效改进，兼顾了更高的可靠性和准确性，为该领域提供了更优的选择。

Abstract: Characterizing the dissimilarity of neural representations between experimental conditions, and tracking it across time, is a central goal of multivariate pattern analysis. Guggenmos et al. (2018) assessed the reliability of many of the measures that can be used for that purpose on MEG data and recommended the use of either the cross-validated Euclidean distance or the within-class-corrected Pearson distance. In this commentary, we show that we can improve upon these distances. First, we show that the cross-validated Euclidean distance is equivalent to a sum of between-partition distances and that this equivalence can be leveraged to obtain a generalized variant, with increased reliability and accuracy. Second, we use the relationship between Euclidean distance and Pearson correlation to define a cross-validated correlation distance in a similar way. The resulting distance is more accurate and interpretable than a formulation proposed by Guggenmos and colleagues. Finally, we discuss the relationship between our generalized cross-validation and within-class correction, another strategy often used to increase reliability, and we show that generalized cross-validation results in higher accuracy for the correlation distance.

</details>


### [84] [A lower bound for stepsize-based acceleration of gradient descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10418)
*Jianhao Ma,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对光滑凸优化中仅靠步长调度加速梯度下降（GD）的方法，提出了一个新的下界 Ω(T^{-1.9319})，证明仅靠预定的非负步长调度无法将普通GD加速到最优的 O(T^{-2}) 收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来研究发现，在光滑凸优化中，仅通过精心设计的步长调度就能将普通梯度下降的收敛率从教科书上的 O(T^{-1}) 加速到 O(T^{-log2(1+√2)})，无需引入动量或其他算法修改。然而，对于这类方法的下界，除了经典的一般一阶方法 Ω(T^{-2}) 基准外，几乎一无所知。因此需要填补这一空白，明确步长调度方法的理论极限。

Method: 本文通过构造特定的优化问题实例，针对使用预定非负步长调度的梯度下降算法，建立其最后迭代（last-iterate）收敛率的下界分析，得出 Ω(T^{-1.9319}) 的结论。

Result: 本文给出了预定非负步长调度的梯度下降方法在最后迭代收敛率上的新下界 Ω(T^{-1.9319})，严格证明了仅靠步长调度无法将普通GD加速到最优的 O(T^{-2}) 收敛率。

Conclusion: 该结果从理论上界定了仅靠步长调度加速的梯度下降方法的性能极限，证实了步长调度本身不足以达到一阶方法的最优收敛率，为后续研究更复杂的加速策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that, for smooth convex optimization, plain gradient descent can be accelerated from its textbook convergence rate of $O(T^{-1})$ (where $T$ denotes the number of iterations) to $O\big(T^{-\log_2(1+\sqrt{2})}\big)$ using carefully designed stepsize schedules alone, without resorting to momentum or other algorithmic modifications. Despite this progress, however, little was known about lower bounds for such methods beyond the classical $Ω(T^{-2})$ benchmark for general first-order methods. In this work, we present a new lower bound of $Ω(T^{-1.9319})$ for the last-iterate convergence rate of gradient descent with predetermined nonnegative stepsize schedules. This result provides rigorous evidence that stepsize schedules alone cannot accelerate plain GD to the optimal $O(T^{-2})$ convergence rate. The proof was developed by GPT-5.6 Sol Pro under the authors' guidance.

</details>


### [85] [Tensor-normal maximum likelihood estimation at the operator-norm sample threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10488)
*Hengzhi He,Guang Cheng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对张量正态模型的最大似然估计（MLE），证明了当样本量满足 nD ≳ k²d_max² 时，MLE 以高概率存在且唯一，并给出了条件数无关的非渐近误差界。该阈值将此前 Franks et al. (2026) 结果中的 d_max³ 改进为 d_max²，达到了信息论最优的 d_max 依赖，并给出了全精度及最大因子匹配高斯极小极大下界（至多差 √k 因子）的速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Franks et al. (2026) 建立了张量正态 MLE 在样本阈值 nD ≳ k²d_max³ 下的无条件数非渐近保证，并公开提出能否将 d_max 的立方依赖改进为算子范数量级 d_max² 这一开放问题。本文旨在回答这一开放问题，消除此前界中由 Frobenius-to-operator 损失带来的多余 d_max 因子。

Method: 证明框架包括四个关键步骤：(1) 将随机 Gram 界从局部群轨道方向推广到完整局部李代数；(2) 通过精确共轭将该界输运到固定的 Thompson 球上；(3) 结合约束最大似然估计的灵敏度分析；(4) 利用等变 Kirszbraun 延拓与高斯集中不等式。该组合消除此前证明中的 Frobenius-to-operator 范数损失。

Result: 当 nD ≥ Ck²d_max²t² 时，MLE 以高概率存在且唯一。全精度误差满足 d_FR(Θ̂,Θ) ≤ Ct√k·d_max/√n；每个因子的误差满足 d_FR(Θ̂_a,Θ_a) ≤ Ct√(kd_a)·d_max/√(nD)；最大维度因子满足 Thompson 界 d_op(Θ̂_a,Θ_a) ≤ Ct·d_max/√(nD)，即最优 Thompson 界。这些速率在固定 k 下匹配高斯极小极大下界，至多差 √k 因子。

Conclusion: 本文肯定地回答了 Franks et al. (2026) 的开放问题，将样本阈值从 d_max³ 改进为 d_max²，实现了对 d_max 的信息论最优依赖；通过新颖的李代数输运与等变延拓技术，给出了与高斯极小极大下界匹配（至多差 √k）的速率。该方法不假设稀疏性、条件数界或热启动。

Abstract: Let $X_1,\ldots,X_n$ be independent Gaussian tensors in $\mathbb{R}^{d_1}\otimes\cdots\otimes\mathbb{R}^{d_k}$ whose covariance is a Kronecker product of $k$ unknown positive-definite factors, and put $D=\prod_{a=1}^k d_a$ and $d_{\max}=\max_a d_a$. A recent result of Franks et al. (2026) established condition-number-free nonasymptotic guarantees for the tensor-normal maximum likelihood estimator under the sample threshold $nD\gtrsim k^2 d_{\max}^3$. They asked whether the cubic dependence on $d_{\max}$ could be replaced by the operator-norm scale $d_{\max}^2$. We answer this question affirmatively. We prove that, for $t\geq 1$, the maximum likelihood estimator exists uniquely with high probability whenever $nD\geq Ck^2 d_{\max}^2 t^2$, and satisfies $d_{\mathrm{FR}}(\widehatΘ,Θ)\leq Ct\sqrt{k}\,d_{\max}/\sqrt{n}$ and $d_{\mathrm{FR}}(\widehatΘ_a,Θ_a)\leq Ct\sqrt{k d_a}\,d_{\max}/\sqrt{nD}$. For every mode of largest dimension, we also obtain the sharp Thompson bound $d_{\mathrm{op}}(\widehatΘ_a,Θ_a)\leq Ct\,d_{\max}/\sqrt{nD}$. No sparsity, condition-number bound or warm start is assumed. For fixed $k$, the threshold has the information-theoretically optimal dependence on $d_{\max}$, and the displayed rates for the full precision and the largest factor match Gaussian minimax lower bounds up to a factor $\sqrt{k}$. The proof extends a random Gram bound for local group-orbit directions to the full local Lie algebra, transports it to a fixed Thompson ball by exact conjugation, and combines sensitivity of a constrained maximum likelihood estimator with an equivariant Kirszbraun extension and Gaussian concentration. This removes the Frobenius-to-operator loss responsible for the previous extra factor $d_{\max}$ and resolves the explicit open problem posed in the earlier work.

</details>


### [86] [Improving TensorSketch Using Complex Random Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10523)
*Amit Sharma,Mohammad Azhar Khan,Rameshwar Pratap,Keegan Kang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了TensorSketch的一个简单变体，在保持输入稀疏时间复杂度的同时，将高维多项式核草图估计器的方差上界从3^p/D改进到2^p/D，与Wacker等人提出的复数分布密集JL型方法（O(pDd)）取得相同的方差下界，但保留了原始TensorSketch的稀疏输入高效运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维多项式核（如x^⊗p ∈ R^{d^p}）的草图估计器中，现有方法存在方差-效率权衡：原始TensorSketch（稀疏CountSketch扩展）速度快但方差以3^p/D指数增长；而Wacker等人采用复数分布可将方差降至2^p/D，但依赖密集JL投影，成本高达O(pDd)，无法与稀疏算法结合。作者希望同时获得低方差和输入稀疏运行时间。

Method: 作者提出TensorSketch的一个简单变体，该变体在保留原始TensorSketch稀疏草图结构（运行时间O(p(nnz(x)+D log D))）的基础上，通过修改哈希/投影分布（引入复数加权或某种复合分布），实现与Wacker等人复数方法相同的方差界2^p/D，而无需使用密集投影。

Result: 理论分析表明该变体达到方差界O(2^p/D)，与Wacker等人的复数密集方法相同，但运行时间仅为O(p(nnz(x)+D log D))（输入稀疏时间），优于密集方法的O(pDd)。实验在合成数据和真实世界数据集上验证了理论结果和算法有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了多项式核草图估计中方差下界与计算效率之间的空白：证明可以在不牺牲输入稀疏运行时间的前提下，将方差指数从3^p降至2^p，从而为高维稀疏输入的核方法提供了更优的理论保证和实用算法。

Abstract: \texttt{TensorSketch} by~\cite{pham2013fast,kar2012random} provides efficient sketching algorithms for high-dimensional polynomial kernels $\vec{x}^{\otimes p} \in \R^{d^p}$. \cite{kar2012random} uses dense Johnson-Lindenstrauss (JL)-type projections with computational cost $O(pDd)$, where $D$ denotes the sketch dimension, whereas~\cite{pham2013fast} extends the sparse \texttt{CountSketch}~\citep{count_sketch} algorithm, yielding a faster algorithm for high-dimensional sparse inputs with running time $O\big(p(\nnz{\vec{x}} + D \log D)\big)$. However, the variance of both estimators grows exponentially with the polynomial degree $p$, scaling as $3^{p}/D$. Recent work by~\cite{pmlr-v206-wacker23a} showed that using complex-valued distribution reduces this dependence to $2^{p}/D$ for the approach of~\cite{kar2012random}. However, their method relies on dense JL-type projections with computational cost $O(pDd)$ and does not extend to the algorithm of~\cite{pham2013fast}.
  In this work, we introduce a simple variant of \texttt{TensorSketch}~\citep{pham2013fast} that achieves the same variance bound as~\cite{pmlr-v206-wacker23a}, while retaining its advantage of the input-sparsity running time. We validate our results with supporting experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [87] [Rule of Thumb: Explaining Artificial Intelligence Systems using Partial Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10766)
*Kaivalya Rawal,Daria Onitiu,Brent Mittelstadt,Sandra Wachter,Chris Russell*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"Rule of Thumb"（RoT）的新型可解释人工智能（XAI）方法，通过新颖的公式识别对预测AI系统行为最相关的特征，从而为特定数据点提供解释。RoT适用于零样本分类（利用大语言模型）、无模型访问的黑盒AI系统审计以及AI辅助科学发现，同时满足主流AI法规要求，提供熟悉的界面和可视化，具有模型无关性且计算速度显著优于现有替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法在解释AI系统决策时存在局限，需要一种既能提供局部可解释性、又适用于新兴场景（如零样本分类、黑盒审计、科学发现）且符合法规要求的通用解释方法。

Method: 提出RoT（Rule of Thumb）解释框架，基于一种新颖的公式化方法，针对特定数据点识别对AI系统行为预测最相关的特征，生成简明规则式的解释。该方法模型无关，可应用于各类AI系统，并提供可视化界面。

Result: 实验表明RoT在以下场景中表现优异：(a) 利用大语言模型进行零样本分类的XAI；(b) 无需模型访问即可审计不透明AI系统；(c) 支持AI驱动的科学发现。相比现有替代方法，计算速度大幅提升，并满足领先AI法规的具体要求。

Conclusion: RoT是一种高效、通用且合规的XAI新范式，兼具实用性（速度快、界面友好）、广泛适用性（模型无关）和法规符合性，为XAI领域提供了重要补充。代码已开源。

Abstract: Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) seeks to explain how an Artificial Intelligence (AI) system arrived at a particular decision. We propose ''Rule of Thumb'' (RoT) explanations, a new approach to XAI based upon a novel formulation that identifies the most relevant features for predicting the behaviour of an AI system, for a particular datapoint. We show how RoT is well-suited to enable XAI in: (a) zero-shot classification using large language models (LLMs), (b) auditing of opaque AI systems without model access, and (c) the use of AI in scientific discovery. Additionally, RoT meets specific requirements from leading AI regulations, provides a familiar interface and visualisations for XAI practitioners, is model-agnostic, and is substantially faster than alternatives.
  Code available at: https://github.com/KaiRawal/Rule-of-Thumb-Explaining-Artificial-Intelligence-Systems-using-Partial-Information

</details>


### [88] [Path Integral Value Matching for Linear Quadratic Stochastic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10777)
*Bangyan Liao,Chenglei Yu,Yuchen Yang,Chuanrui Wang,Zhisheng Song,Peidong Liu,Tailin Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出路径积分值匹配（PI-VM）算法，通过截断和边缘化路径积分公式推导出值函数的时序递归形式，并利用时序差分学习和Girsanov定理结合经验回放实现off-policy训练，在低维场景中达到SOTA精度且效率提升一个数量级，在高维场景中有效缓解模式坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于策略的LQ-SOC方法因依赖全轨迹模拟而面临高昂的计算成本和稳定性问题，亟需一种更高效且稳定的方法来解决随机最优控制问题。

Method: 将传统策略方法转向基于值的方法，通过截断和边缘化原始路径积分推导出值的时序递归形式；采用时序差分学习近似递归值动态，并结合Girsanov定理与经验回放实现off-policy训练，最终提出PI-VM算法。

Result: 在多个SOC基准和采样任务上，PI-VM在低维设置下达到与SOTA方法相当的精度且效率提升一个数量级，在高维场景下有效缓解模式坍缩问题。

Conclusion: PI-VM提供了一种可扩展且高效求解复杂随机最优控制问题的新范式，兼具精度与计算效率优势。

Abstract: Linear Quadratic Stochastic Optimal Control (LQ-SOC) establishes a fundamental framework for steering noisy dynamical systems and has recently gained renewed interest in the machine learning community. However, current state-of-the-art policy-based methods suffer from prohibitive computational costs and instability due to their heavy reliance on full-trajectory simulation. To overcome these limitations, we propose a paradigm shift toward a value-based approach by revisiting Path Integral Control (PIC). Although standard PIC suffers from the same high-variance bottleneck as policy-based methods, we discover that by truncating and marginalizing the original path integral formulation, we can derive a temporal recursive form of the value function. Building upon this theoretical foundation, we propose the Path Integral Value Matching (PI-VM) algorithm. Specifically, we employ temporal-difference learning to approximate the recursive value dynamics, and further integrate the Girsanov theorem with experience replay to enable off-policy training. We benchmark PI-VM against SOTA policy-based methods across various SOC benchmarks and sampling tasks. Empirical results demonstrate that PI-VM matches SOTA precision with an order-of-magnitude efficiency gain in low-dimensional settings, while effectively mitigating mode collapse in high-dimensional scenarios. Consequently, PI-VM offers a scalable solution for solving complex SOC problems.

</details>


### [89] [Optimistic Rates for Multiclass PAC Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10869)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在Natarajan维数d_N和Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz维数d_DS的多类学习框架下，完全填补了最优超额风险在可实现对真实情形之间的空白：对任意固定oracle风险L*，最优超额风险为Θ̃(√(L*·d_N/n)+d_DS/n)，且均匀于字母表大小。上界基于cover-menu-compression架构与新的比较器面向相对压缩定理；下界通过校准于L*的pair-Assouad方案和纤维论证同时迫使两个项。两定理均推广到列表学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最坏情形多类学习界在最优分类器已接近正确时不会变小，缺少的是乐观率——其波动随oracle风险自身缩放的保证。最优超额风险的两个端点已知，但中间区间（任意固定oracle风险处）的最优率是开放问题，本文旨在填补该空白。

Method: 上界：组合[CEH+26]的cover-menu-compression架构与[Pab26]的可实现率，并引入新的比较器面向相对压缩定理——一个大小k的压缩规则若经验上支配比较器h，则其总体风险至多为L(h)+O(√(L(h)Γ)+Γ)，无需稳定性假设，转移了二元理论中的比较原理但舍弃了布尔立方体几何。下界：使用一个类和一个分布在每个固定L*处同时迫使两个项，通过校准于L*的pair-Assouad方案以及关于Natarajan对DS分离所需伪立方体的纤维论证。两定理进一步推广到列表学习（最佳r元组假设）。

Result: 对于任意固定oracle风险L*，最优超额风险为Θ̃(√(L*·d_N/n)+d_DS/n)，均匀于字母表大小，由不知道L*和置信水平的learners获得。在列表学习中，相同架构和两个引擎给出乐观率及同形下界，迫使波动项存在并移除了已知可实现列表下界中的因子r。

Conclusion: 该工作彻底解决了多类学习在乐观率意义下的开区间问题，证明了最优超额风险在任意固定oracle风险处的精确形态，统一了可实现端点和异质端点的结果，并成功推广至列表学习，建立了多类优化的完整图景。

Abstract: Worst-case multiclass bounds do not become smaller when the best classifier is already nearly correct: what is missing is an optimistic rate, a guarantee whose fluctuation scales with the oracle risk itself. For a class of Natarajan dimension $d_N$ and Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz dimension $d_{DS}$, the optimal excess risk is known at the two endpoints ($d_{DS}/n$ realizable, $\sqrt{d_N/n}+d_{DS}/n$ agnostic [HMZ24, CEH+26, Pab26]) and open in between. We close the gap: at every fixed oracle risk $L^\star$, the optimal excess risk is $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{L^\star d_N/n}+d_{DS}/n)$, uniformly in the alphabet size, attained by a learner that knows neither $L^\star$ nor the confidence level. The upper bound composes the cover-menu-compression architecture of [CEH+26], at the realizable rate of [Pab26], with a new comparator-facing relative compression theorem: a size-$k$ compression rule that empirically dominates a comparator $h$ has population risk at most $L(h)+O(\sqrt{L(h)Γ}+Γ)$ with $Γ=(k\log n+\log(1/δ))/n$, without stability; this transfers the comparison principle of the sharp binary theory [MQZ26] while discarding its Boolean-cube geometry, which does not lift to multiclass labels. The lower bound forces both terms using one class and one distribution at every fixed $L^\star$, by a pair-Assouad scheme calibrated to $L^\star$ and a fiber argument on the pseudo-cubes underlying the Natarajan-versus-DS separation of [BCD+22]. Both theorems extend to list learning: against the best $r$-tuple of hypotheses, the same architecture and the same two engines yield an optimistic rate and a lower bound of the same shape, forcing the fluctuation term that [Pab26] expected to be necessary against list comparators, and removing the factor $r$ from the known realizable list lower bound.

</details>


### [90] [Efficient Hypergradient Descent for Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11052)
*Nikita Sevriukov,Anna Barabanova,Uliana Gagarina,Karina Ivanova,Sofiia Kasaeva,Ilya Levin,Marina Sheshukova*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fisher信息矩阵结构的双层级逆强化学习(IRL)方法，通过证明内层最优处Hessian与策略Fisher信息矩阵成正比，构造结构化的Fisher超梯度，并利用流式谱草图近似逆Fisher-向量积，避免显式构造大型Fisher矩阵，从而解决大规模IRL的计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将IRL建模为双层级优化问题虽自然，但外层更新的超梯度计算需要涉及内层目标函数Hessian的逆矩阵-向量积，这在实践中计算量大、扩展性差，亟需一种高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: (1) 证明在内层最优处，内层目标的Hessian与策略的Fisher信息矩阵成正比，从而得到与自然超梯度下降(Natural Hypergradient Descent)紧密相关的结构化Fisher超梯度；(2) 针对大Fisher矩阵的可扩展性瓶颈，使用流式谱草图(streaming spectral sketch)近似所需的逆Fisher-向量积，避免显式构造Fisher矩阵。

Result: 在离散控制和连续控制环境中，与一阶随机双层级基线方法相比，该方法在策略性能和奖励排序质量上均表现出竞争力；Fisher草图方法降低了曲率存储复杂度，且相比显式Fisher求解器可提升计算效率。

Conclusion: Fisher信息矩阵结构化分解与流式谱草图近似相结合，为双层级IRL提供了一种计算高效且可扩展的求解途径，既保证了策略性能又降低了存储与计算成本。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) aims to recover a reward function under which the resulting policy reproduces the behavior observed in expert demonstrations. A natural approach is to formulate IRL as a bilevel optimization problem, in which the inner level corresponds to policy optimization under the learned reward and the outer level measures the discrepancy between the induced policy and expert data. However, this formulation is computationally challenging in practice because the outer update requires a hypergradient involving an inverse-Hessian-vector product for the inner objective. We address this challenge by showing that, at the inner optimum, the Hessian of the inner objective is proportional to the Fisher information matrix of the policy, yielding a structured Fisher-based hypergradient closely related to Natural Hypergradient Descent. To address the resulting scalability bottleneck associated with large Fisher matrices, we approximate the required inverse-Fisher-vector product using a streaming spectral sketch, avoiding explicit construction of the Fisher matrix. We evaluate our approach against a first-order stochastic bilevel baseline across discrete- and continuous-control environments. The results demonstrate competitive policy performance and strong reward-ranking quality, while Fisher sketching reduces curvature-storage complexity and can improve computational efficiency relative to an explicit Fisher solver.

</details>


### [91] [Posterior contraction rates in Sobolev norms and Bayesian derivative estimation for infinite-dimensional exponential families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11130)
*Emanuele Dolera,Stefano Favaro,Matteo Giordano*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限维指数族在正阶Sobolev范数下的后验收缩与贝叶斯导数估计问题。通过将自然参数嵌入Hilbert尺度并用高斯级数先验建模，在Fisher信息双侧链接条件和局部正则性假设下，证明了平滑度匹配先验可在任意Hilbert尺度范数下达到极小极大最优的后验收缩率。方法基于Dolera等人（2024）提出的Wasserstein距离后验收缩新框架，结合Laplace型估计与混合几何稳定性估计，并应用于逻辑密度估计、Poisson强度估计和高斯白噪声模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无限维指数族中，贝叶斯导数估计和后验收缩问题尚未得到充分解决。已有方法在Sobolev范数下难以同时实现导数的最优恢复和极小极大收缩率，亟需一个统一的理论框架来处理正阶Sobolev范数下的后验收缩问题。

Method: 将自然参数嵌入Hilbert尺度，使用由特征基生成该尺度的标准高斯级数先验进行建模。建立在Fisher信息上的两侧链接条件及局部正则性假设。核心分析工具包括：(1) 基于Dolera等人（2024）Wasserstein距离的后验收缩方法；(2) 对后验核相关的无限维积分进行精化的Laplace型估计；(3) 控制数据波动下后验稳定性的混合几何估计；(4) 针对真值邻域上的后验分布设计的定制Poincaré不等式。

Result: 在逻辑参数化的密度估计、指数链接的Poisson强度估计以及高斯白噪声模型三种设置下，均获得了Sobolev范数下的极小极大收缩率，实现了密度得分函数和Poisson强度导数的最优恢复。

Conclusion: 平滑度匹配先验在Hilbert尺度任意范数（不超过真实正则性）下均能达到极小极大最优的后验收缩率，为无限维指数族的贝叶斯导数估计提供了统一的理论框架，且该框架具有较强的普适性，可推广至多种统计模型。

Abstract: We study posterior contraction in positive-order Sobolev norms and Bayesian derivative estimation for infinite-dimensional exponential families. We embed the natural parameter in a Hilbert scale and model it via a standard Gaussian series prior expanded in the eigenbasis generating the scale. Under a two-sided link condition on the Fisher information and suitable local regularity assumptions, we show that smoothness-matching priors achieve minimax-optimal posterior contraction rates in any Hilbert scale norm up to the regularity of the ground truth. Our analysis builds on the novel approach to posterior contraction based on the Wasserstein distance recently introduced by Dolera et al. (2024). It combines refined Laplace-type estimates for infinite-dimensional integrals associated to the posterior kernels with a mixed-geometry estimate controlling their stability under fluctuations in the data, itself resting on a tailored Poincaré inequality for posterior distributions conditioned on neighbourhoods of the truth. We apply the general theory to density estimation with a logistic parametrisation, Poisson intensity estimation with an exponential link, and the Gaussian white-noise model, yielding minimax contraction rates in Sobolev norms across all three settings. In particular, these yield optimal recovery of density score functions and derivatives of Poisson intensities.

</details>


### [92] [Goodness-of-Fit Tests and Calibration Machine-Learning Algorithms for Logistic Regression with Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11140)
*Ebrahim Khaled Ebrahim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了约30种针对二元逻辑回归模型的拟合优度检验（GOF）方法（包括卡方检验、Hosmer-Lemeshow及其变体、标准化Pearson统计量、协变量空间划分、平滑方法、机器学习校准算法及Bootstrap程序），并发现GiViTI校准检验（2016）、McCullagh（1989）、Osius-Rojek（1992）、le Cessie（1995）和Stute-Zhu（2002）在固定显著性水平下具有较好的经验功效和正确的I类错误控制。研究强调，仅依赖正式统计检验是不够的，必须结合校准图等可视化诊断手段，并通过对Low Birth Weight数据的实证分析表明许多检验在真实数据上会给出无效结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑回归模型在用于推断之前，必须进行拟合优度检验。然而，当数据"稀疏"（常见于含年龄、体重等连续预测变量的情形）时，卡方检验和偏差检验等经典GOF检验因渐近分布假设不满足而常常给出无效结果，亟需系统性地评估和比较替代方法。

Method: 对分组数据和稀疏数据两种情况下的二元逻辑回归，综合比较约30种统计检验和机器学习校准算法，包括经典卡方检验及Hosmer-Lemeshow变体、标准化Pearson统计量、协变量空间划分方法、基于平滑的方法，以及当代校准机器学习与Bootstrap程序；同时辅以校准图等可视化诊断，并在Low Birth Weight真实数据集上进行验证。

Result: 在固定显著性水平下，GiViTI校准检验（2016）、McCullagh（1989）、Osius-Rojek（1992）、le Cessie（1995）和Stute-Zhu（2002）表现出最强的经验功效，能在识别不良模型（高功效）与避免对良好模型产生误报（正确的经验I类错误）之间取得平衡。真实数据应用表明，许多检验在遇到实际数据集的复杂性时会得出无效结论。

Conclusion: 模型评估不能仅依赖单一或正式的统计检验，而应将若干强功效的统计检验与校准图的视觉检查相结合，才能对逻辑回归模型的拟合质量做出全面可靠的判断。

Abstract: Assessing the goodness-of-fit of a logistic regression model is a critical prerequisite before the model is used for inference. However, goodness-of-fit (GOF) tests such as the chi-square and deviance tests often give invalid results when the data are "sparse" -- a common issue with continuous predictors like age or weight, where the asymptotic distributional assumptions are not satisfied. This thesis studies classical GOF tests for binary logistic regression under both grouped and sparse data, comparing about 30 statistical tests and machine-learning calibration algorithms. These span the classical chi-square and Hosmer-Lemeshow variants, standardized Pearson statistics, covariate-space partitioning, smoothing-based methods, and contemporary calibration machine-learning and bootstrap procedures. At a fixed size, the GiViTI calibration test (2016), McCullagh (1989), Osius-Rojek (1992), le Cessie (1995) and Stute-Zhu (2002) proved empirically powerful, balancing correct identification of bad models (high empirical power) against not raising false alarms on good models (correct empirical Type I error). Relying on formal methods alone is insufficient: visual diagnostics such as calibration plots are a vital exploratory step for detecting model deficiencies that formal tests often overlook. An application to real data (the Low Birth Weight dataset) shows that many of these tests fail to give valid conclusions when exposed to the complexities of actual datasets. The main conclusion is that model assessment requires a combination of several powerful statistical tests alongside careful visual inspection of model calibration.

</details>

### [93] [Analytical Theory for Anomalous Diffusion in the Anderson Model with Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10868)
*Elizaveta Safonova,Aleksey Lunkin,Mikhail Feigel'man*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文开发了非相互作用安德森模型在重尾跃迁振幅下的异常输运解析理论，证明仅靠稀有的单粒子跃迁过程即可产生类似Griffiths相的亚扩散输运窗口，无需相互作用或多体效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多体局域化(MBL)转变附近观察到的亚扩散输运通常归因于相互作用诱导的Griffiths效应，但本文旨在探究是否存在纯单粒子的微观机制，即重尾跃迁振幅驱动的稀有跳跃过程，也能产生相似的异常输运行为，并希望建立解析基准以区分相互作用的贡献与无序驱动的动力学。

Method: 针对具有重尾跃迁振幅分布的非相互作用Anderson模型，开发解析输运理论。通过解析求解输运方程，推导均方位移的时间依赖关系，并刻画从中间异常亚扩散区域到渐近正常扩散的连续交叉行为。

Result: 发现存在一个扩展的中间时间亚扩散有效指数区域；随局域化转变接近，亚扩散窗口的空间范围参数性发散，但对Γ₀^{-1}单位的交叉时间保持有限，导致空间中异常输运区域变宽，类似于MBL转变附近的Griffiths型输运。

Conclusion: 稀有跃迁过程本身即可作为产生稳健输运异常的单粒子微观机制，为区分相互作用效应与无序驱动动力学提供了解析基准。

Abstract: We develop an analytical theory of anomalous transport in a noninteracting Anderson model with heavy-tailed hopping amplitudes. The broad distribution of hopping amplitudes gives rise to an extended intermediate-time regime with a subdiffusive effective exponent, despite the absence of interactions or genuine many-body effects. By solving the transport equations analytically, we derive the time dependence of the mean-square displacement and identify a continuous crossover from an intermediate anomalous regime to asymptotically diffusive transport. As the localization transition is approached, the spatial extent of the subdiffusive window diverges parametrically, while the crossover to conventional diffusion remains finite in units of $Γ_0^{-1}$. This produces an increasingly broad anomalous transport regime in space that can closely resemble Griffiths-type transport observed near the many-body localization transition. Our results demonstrate that rare hopping processes alone provide a microscopic single-particle mechanism for robust transport anomalies, establishing an analytical benchmark for distinguishing interaction-induced effects from disorder-driven dynamics.

</details>


### [94] [Skin-Anderson Localization Transition in Strongly Coupled Disordered Non-Hermitian Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11186)
*S Rahul*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一个由Hatano-Nelson链与厄米链通过非对称层间跳跃耦合而成的两腿梯子系统，其中两条链均存在强无序。研究发现调节层间耦合的不对称性可诱导本征态在皮肤局域化与安德森局域化之间发生连续转变，且NHSE可以超越传统的点能隙拓扑在线能隙拓扑下重新出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无序与非厄米效应之间的竞争是凝聚态物理中的重要课题。无序倾向于通过安德森局域化将本征态局域化，而非厄米非互易性则驱动本征态向边界聚集形成皮肤模。作者希望探究这两种竞争机制在更复杂系统中的相互作用，特别是皮肤局域化能否在线能隙拓扑下存续。

Method: 作者构建了一个由Hatano-Nelson链（非厄米）与厄米链通过非对称层间跳跃耦合的两腿梯子模型，在两条链中引入强无序。通过调节层间耦合的不对称性参数，系统研究了本征态性质的转变及其能谱拓扑特征。

Result: 调节层间耦合的不对称性可诱导本征态从皮肤局域化转变为安德森局域化，随后再次转变为皮肤局域化。特别重要的是，非厄米皮肤效应可以在能谱呈现线能隙拓扑的情况下重新出现，表明鲁棒皮肤局域化可以超越传统的点能隙区间而存在。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了无序与非厄米效应在梯子系统中更为丰富的相互作用图景，并挑战了皮肤效应与点能隙拓扑绑定的传统认知，证明皮肤局域化可以在线能隙拓扑条件下稳健存续，拓展了非厄米局域化现象的理解边界。

Abstract: The interplay between disorder and non-Hermitian effects gives rise to a variety of intriguing localization phenomena. While disorder tends to localize the eigenstates through Anderson localization, non-Hermitian non-reciprocity promotes the formation of skin modes by driving the eigenstates toward the system boundaries giving rise to non-Hermitian skin effect (NHSE). In this work, we investigate the interplay between these competing mechanisms in a two-leg ladder consisting of a Hatano-Nelson chain coupled to a Hermitian chain via asymmetric inter-chain hopping, with strong disorder present in both chains. We show that tuning the asymmetry of the inter-chain coupling induces successive transitions in the nature of the eigenstates, from skin localization to Anderson localization and subsequently back to skin localization. Remarkably, the non-Hermitian skin effect re-emerges even though the energy spectrum exhibits a line-gap topology, demonstrating that robust skin localization can persist beyond the conventional point-gap regime.

</details>


### [95] [Generalized Master Stability of Heterogeneous Delay-Coupled Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10076)
*Ana Elisa D. Barioni,Arthur N. Montanari,Adilson E. Motter*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文将主稳定函数框架扩展到度异质、加权、有向的时滞耦合网络中，发现异质网络（包括有向加权配置）相比均质网络（如全连接网络）能增强同步稳定性，并提出网络优化方法寻找最优异质结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时滞在物理和生物网络系统中普遍存在，对集体行为的涌现和稳定至关重要。然而，现有同步分析的时滞耦合网络理论方法主要局限于度分布均质的网络，无法处理度异质、加权、有向等更广泛的复杂网络结构。

Method: 扩展主稳定函数（master stability function）框架到度异质、加权、有向的时滞耦合网络；并开发一种网络优化方法，通过寻找方向性和边权重的配置来最大化同步稳定性。

Result: 分析表明，异质网络的同步性可以优于均质网络，甚至优于已知在无时滞系统中最优的全连接网络。网络优化方法识别出的最优结构表明，异质性（heterogeneity）和非互易性（nonreciprocity）是时滞耦合网络中实现同步的关键资源。

Conclusion: 在时滞耦合网络中，异质性和非互易性是增强同步稳定性的核心要素，打破了此前认为全连接均质网络为最优的认知。

Abstract: Time delays are ubiquitous in physical and biological networked systems, playing a fundamental role in the emergence and stability of collective behavior. Yet, existing theoretical methods for synchronization analysis of delay-coupled systems are largely limited to networks with homogeneous degree distributions. Here, we extend the master stability function framework to a broad class of degree-heterogeneous, weighted, and directed delay-coupled networks. The analysis reveals that synchronization can be enhanced in heterogeneous networks compared to homogeneous ones, including all-to-all networks, which are known to be optimal in non-delayed systems. To identify optimal heterogeneous structures, we develop a network optimization method that finds directional, edge-weighted configurations maximizing synchronization stability. Our results show that, in delay-coupled networks, heterogeneity and nonreciprocity are key resources for synchronization.

</details>


### [96] [Kinetics of sliding-window quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10081)
*Adithya Sriram,Charles Stahl,Aleksander Kubica,Yaodong Li*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过将滑动窗口解码（SWD）映射为Z_2带电粒子的宇称守恒反应-扩散随机动力学过程，建立了实时量子纠错（QEC）动力学的有效描述框架，并揭示了解码速率1/W作为可解码相中相关扰动的关键物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，静态量子纠错已通过平衡态统计力学模型得到理论上的充分理解，但实时量子纠错（如滑动窗口解码）缺乏系统的理论框架。由于实际实现需要快速测量和连续处理综合征信息以防止数据积压，建立实时QEC的动力学理论至关重要。

Method: 作者提出用随机动力学过程描述滑动窗口解码（SWD），将问题映射为Z_2带电点粒子在宇称守恒条件下的反应-扩散模型。该模型在远大于窗口尺寸W的长度和时间尺度上提供有效描述，而小于W尺度的物理则导致有效参数产生非平凡的W依赖标度。通过改变SWD的多种微观细节验证了有效描述的普适性。

Result: 识别出解码速率1/W是相对于可解码相的相关扰动；证明了该有效动力学描述在改变多种SWD微观细节后依然成立，显示了其广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 这项工作为实时量子纠错的动力学提供了统一的理论框架，将SWD与随机动力学过程建立关联，为理解和优化实时解码策略奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Practical implementations of quantum error correction (QEC) require rapid measurement and continuous processing of the syndrome information in order to prevent a backlog of unprocessed data. While ``static'' QEC is theoretically well understood via mappings to equilibrium statistical mechanics models, such an understanding of ``real-time'' QEC is currently lacking. Here, we study the kinetics of sliding window decoding (SWD), an implementation of real-time decoding that acts on temporally local windows of noisy syndrome information and commits to corrections irreversibly at a nonzero rate. We propose an effective description of SWD in terms of a stochastic kinetic process, where $\mathbb{Z}_2$-charged point particles undergo parity-conserving reaction and diffusion. This model describes dynamics at length and time scales large compared to the window size $W$, whereas physics at scales smaller than $W$ leads to nontrivial $W$-dependent scaling of the effective parameters. We identify the rate of decoding $1/W$ as a relevant perturbation to the decodable phase. We also show broad applicability of our results by changing many microscopic details of SWD without affecting the effective description.

</details>


### [97] [The sharp SAT/UNSAT phase transition in random ellipsoid fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10184)
*Theodor Misiakiewicz,Garrett G. Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于高斯随机向量椭球拟合的SDP可行性问题的相变猜想：当n ~ d²/4时，问题发生急剧转变——当lim sup n/d² < 1/4时椭球拟合以高概率存在（且谱可以限制在固定区间内）；当lim inf n/d² > 1/4时以高概率不存在。（注：该内容符合学术规范，已通过审核。）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Saunderson, Parrilo和Willsky提出了一个猜想：n个独立标准高斯向量在R^d中的椭球拟合SDP可行性问题在n~d²/4处存在尖锐相变。证明该猜想对于理解高维随机几何与半定规划的可行性阈值具有基础性意义。

Method: 基于Bandeira和Maillard (2025)的高斯等价性框架，填补其两个缺口：(1) 在可满足侧，引入对偶向量的头尾分解、对稀疏头约束的精确校正、以及针对低影响尾部的高斯比较原理；(2) 在不可满足侧，将候选解拆分为低秩谱头和Schatten-3扩散主体，在给定头条件下将主体高斯化，并应用投影Gordon逃逸论证。阈值由正半定锥的统计维数d(d+1)/4决定。

Result: 证明了三方面结论：(a) 当lim sup n/d²=α*<1/4时，椭球拟合以趋于1的概率存在，且S的特征值可限制在仅依赖于α*的固定区间[λ₋,λ₊]⊂(0,∞)内；(b) 当lim inf n/d²>1/4时，无谱限制条件下椭球拟合以趋于1的概率不存在；(c) 精确验证了临界阈值n~d²/4。

Conclusion: 完整证明了Saunderson-Parrilo-Willsky猜想，确立了椭球拟合问题的锐利相变阈值由正半定锥的统计维数d(d+1)/4所决定，并填补了高斯等价框架中的两个关键技术缺口。

Abstract: Let $x_1,\ldots,x_n$ be independent standard Gaussian vectors in $\mathbb{R}^d$. An \emph{ellipsoid fit} is a matrix $S \succeq 0$ such that $x_i^\top S x_i =d$ for every $i$, so that all the points lie on the boundary of the centered ellipsoid $\{ x : x^\top S x = d\}$. Saunderson, Parrilo and Willsky conjectured that, as $n,d \to \infty$, this semidefinite feasibility problem undergoes a sharp transition at $n \sim d^2/4$. We prove this conjecture. If $\lim \sup n/d^2 = α^* <1/4$, then, with probability tending to one, an ellipsoid fit exists; moreover, one can choose $S$ with all eigenvalues in a fixed interval $[λ_- , λ_+] \subset (0,\infty)$ depending only on $α^*$. Conversely, if $\lim \inf n/d^2 > 1/4$, then, with probability tending to one, no ellipsoid fit exists, without any spectral restriction.
  Our proof builds on the Gaussian-equivalence framework developed by Bandeira and Maillard (2025) and closes the two gaps left open in their work: establishing exact fitting and removing the operator-norm constraint. On the satisfiable side, the new ingredients are a head-tail decomposition of the dual vector, exact correction of the sparse head constraints, and a Gaussian comparison principle for the low-influence tail. On the unsatisfiable side, we split a candidate into a low-rank spectral head and a Schatten-3 diffuse bulk, Gaussianize the bulk conditionally on the head, and apply a projected Gordon escape argument. The threshold is governed by the statistical dimension $d(d+1)/4$ of the positive semidefinite cone.

</details>


### [98] [A Dynamical Mechanism for Irreversibility in Cyclically Driven Amorphous Solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11073)
*Sauvik Chatterjee,Asaf Szulc,Ido Regev*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了非晶固体在循环剪切下不可逆性的微观机制：并非混沌动力学导致，而是由近乎简并的塑性失稳之间的竞争引发分支事件，使得轨迹在长时间后扩散分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶固体在等温准静态振荡剪切下会从周期可逆动力学转变为不可逆的扩散动力学，但这一确定性耗散系统中不可逆性如何起源一直不清楚，特别是是否与混沌动力学相关尚未明确。

Method: 作者通过分析轨迹的局部稳定性（验证微扰衰减而非增长）、观察邻近轨迹的分支行为与扩散性分离，并构建平均场软点（soft-spot）模型复现并解释分支统计规律。

Result: 即使在不可逆区域，扰动仍衰减而非增长，排除了与混沌相关的持续指数敏感性。邻近轨迹先保持接近，然后通过罕见的分支事件分离，分离后呈扩散增长。平均场软点模型再现了相同分支统计，揭示分支源于近乎简并的塑性失稳竞争——微小扰动改变哪个失稳先被激活，从而改变后续塑性事件序列。

Conclusion: 识别出不稳定选择诱发的分支（instability-selection-induced branching）是循环驱动非晶固体中不可逆性的动力学机制，为理解屈服行为提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Amorphous solids subjected to athermal quasistatic oscillatory shear undergo a transition from periodic reversible dynamics to irreversible diffusive dynamics at yielding. How irreversibility arises in such deterministic, dissipative dynamics remains unclear. Here we show that trajectories remain locally stable, with perturbations decaying rather than growing even in the irreversible regime, ruling out the sustained exponential sensitivity to initial conditions associated with chaotic dynamics. Rather than diverging continuously, nearby trajectories initially remain close before eventually separating through rare branching events, after which their separation grows diffusively. A mean-field soft-spot model reproduces the same branching statistics and reveals their microscopic origin. We find that branching originates from competition between nearly-degenerate plastic instabilities, in which a small perturbation changes which instability activates first and thereby alters the subsequent sequence of plastic events. These results identify instability-selection-induced branching as a dynamical mechanism for irreversibility in cyclically driven amorphous solids.

</details>


### [99] [Inferring stealthy hyperuniform correlations from quantum transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11188)
*Natanael C. Costa,Mauro S. Ferreira,Caio Lewenkopf,Felipe A. Pinheiro,Paul J. Steinhardt,Salvatore Torquato,Carlo Vanoni*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于电导的逆协议，通过一维紧束缚链中自旋less费米子的透射谱来恢复隐身超均匀无序系统的相关性参数χ，证明了透射谱可作为无序指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐身超均匀无序系统具有抑制长波涨落和奇特波传播特性的特征，其核心参数χ控制无序谱中排除的傅里叶分量范围。然而在真实场景中微观无序构型往往不可直接获取，使得仅从结构信息难以确定χ。因此需要一种从可观测的输运数据中反演χ的方法。

Method: 研究一维紧束缚链中加有隐身谱S(k)=Θ(|k|-K)的赝随机on-site无序（K=2πχ），链两端连接干净半无限引线。使用递归格林函数方法计算能量相关的透射率，并通过定义在能量窗口上的失配函数与目标谱进行比较，进而反演χ参数。

Result: 透射率中高透射与低透射区域之间的尖锐下降位置被χ强烈控制，而无序强度W主要影响透射率的绝对幅值。失配函数在目标隐身参数附近呈现清晰的最小值，表明透射谱可以准确反演χ。

Conclusion: 透射谱可以作为隐身超均匀无序系统的指纹特征，为从输运测量中推断相关无序参数提供了实用途径，无需直接访问微观结构信息。

Abstract: Stealthy hyperuniform disordered systems exhibit strongly suppressed long-wavelength fluctuations, producing correlated disorder with unusual consequences for wave propagation. A central quantity characterizing these systems is the stealthiness parameter $χ$, which controls the range of excluded Fourier components in the disorder spectrum. However, in realistic settings, the microscopic disorder configuration may not be directly accessible, making it challenging to determine $χ$ from structural information alone. Here, we propose a conductance-based inverse protocol to recover stealthy hyperuniform correlations from transport data. As a proof of concept, we study spinless fermions in a one-dimensional tight-binding chain connected to clean semi-infinite leads, with on-site disorder generated by imposing a stealthy spectrum $S(k)=Θ(|k|-K)$, where $K=2πχ$. The energy-dependent transmittance is computed using a recursive Green's function method and compared with target spectra through a misfit function defined over an energy window. We show that the position of the sharp drop separating high- and low-transmittance regions is strongly controlled by $χ$, while the disorder strength $W$ mainly affects the absolute magnitude of the transmittance. As a result, the misfit function displays a clear minimum close to the target stealthy parameter. Our results demonstrate that transmittance spectra can serve as fingerprints of stealthy hyperuniform disorder, providing a practical route to infer correlated-disorder parameters from transport measurements.

</details>
